# AGENTS.md

Guidance for AI agents and cloud development environments working on HARDGATE.

## Cursor Cloud specific instructions

### Overview

HARDGATE is a zero-dependency static SPA (`index.html` + classic JS modules) served by a small Node HTTP server (`scripts/server.mjs`). There is no build step, no Docker, and no database.

### Agent skills (Claude / Cursor)

This repo ships [addyosmani/agent-skills](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills) for workflow routing (spec → plan → build → test → review → ship).

| Location | Purpose |
|----------|---------|
| `.agents/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` | Primary install (via `npx skills add addyosmani/agent-skills`) |
| `.cursor/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` | Cursor Agent discovery |
| `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` | Claude Code / Copilot skills directory |
| `.cursor/rules/agent-skills.mdc` | Always-on routing rule — start with `using-agent-skills` |
| `.claude/commands/` | Slash commands (`/spec`, `/plan`, `/build`, `/test`, `/review`, `/ship`, …) |
| `agent-skills-references/` | Shared checklists linked from skills |
| `skills-lock.json` | Installed skill versions + hashes |

**Claude Code plugin (local):** `claude --plugin-dir /path/to/hardgate-main` (uses `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`).

**Refresh from upstream:** `npx skills add addyosmani/agent-skills -y` then re-copy to `.cursor/skills/` and `.claude/skills/` if needed.

**HARDGATE defaults when using skills:** run `npm test` before merge; bump `build-stamp.js` + `sw.js` together for deploy; follow tab conventions in this file for UI work.

### Services

| Service | Command | Port | Required? |
|---------|---------|------|-----------|
| HARDGATE dev server | `npm start` | 10000 (or `$PORT`) | Yes — serves static files + `/api/proxy` |
| Browser | Open `http://localhost:10000` | — | Yes — for UI testing |

Start the server in a tmux session so it survives across commands:

```bash
tmux -f /exec-daemon/tmux.portal.conf new-session -d -s hardgate-dev-server -c /workspace -- npm start
```

### Lint / test / run

| Task | Command | Notes |
|------|---------|-------|
| Unit + integration tests | `npm test` | Offline gate; full suite chain (includes daemon, CCXT executor, macro-feeds); no network required |
| Deploy cache check | `npm run check:prod` | Compare local `sw.js` `HG_CACHE` vs `HARDGATE_SITE` (default Render); LIVE vs STALE |
| Live data smoke test | `npm run test:data-layer` | Optional; Binance legs skip on HTTP 451 / timeout; exits 0 |
| Additional suites | `node tests/test-<name>.mjs` | See README Tests section |
| Lint | *(none)* | No ESLint or formatter configured |

### Optional production env vars

Not required for local dev or core UI:

| Variable | Purpose |
|----------|---------|
| `PORT` | Server port (default `10000`) |
| `EXECUTE_BACKEND_URL` / `EXECUTE_WEBHOOK_URL` | Live bracket orders via `/api/execute` (forwarding proxy) |
| `EXECUTE_CCXT_EXCHANGE` + `EXECUTE_CCXT_API_KEY` + `EXECUTE_CCXT_SECRET` | In-process CCXT executor (Bybit / Binance / Delta) — takes priority over `EXECUTE_BACKEND_URL` |
| `EXECUTE_CCXT_SANDBOX`, `EXECUTE_CCXT_PASSWORD`, `EXECUTE_RISK_PCT`, `EXECUTE_CCXT_DEFAULT_TYPE` | CCXT sandbox mode, exchange password, default 1% risk sizing when qty omitted, override `defaultType` |
| `CCXT_MARKET_EXCHANGE`, `CCXT_MARKET_SYMBOLS` | Public CCXT desk at `/api/ccxt/desk` (default `binanceusdm`, BTC+ETH funding) — no API keys |
| `HEY_LENS_API_URL`, `HEY_LENS_AUTHORS` | Hey/Lens social desk at `/api/hey/desk` (default `https://api.lens.xyz/graphql`, authors `stani`) — no wallet |
| `HARDGATE_URL` | Daemon / CI Puppeteer target (default `http://127.0.0.1:10000/` or `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL`) |
| `HARDGATE_SCAN_MS` | Daemon scan interval (default 15 min) |
| `HARDGATE_DAEMON_DRY_RUN=1` | Run daemon loop without CCXT orders |
| `HARDGATE_STATE_FILE` | JSON conviction persistence path (default `hardgate-daemon-state.json`) |
| `HARDGATE_KILL_SWITCH` | Manual trading halt (`1` / `true` blocks CCXT execute + daemon orders + OMNIGOLD XM live sends) |
| `HARDGATE_KILL_SWITCH_PCT` | Auto halt when session PnL % falls below threshold (e.g. `-2` for −2%) |
| `HARDGATE_TRADING_HALT` | Alias for manual halt (same as `HARDGATE_KILL_SWITCH`) |
| `EXECUTE_TWAP_SLICES` | Split CCXT orders into N TWAP slices (default `1` = single order) |
| `EXECUTE_TWAP_INTERVAL_MS` | Delay between TWAP slices in ms (default `0`) |
| `EXECUTE_NAV_USD` | Account NAV for budget checker (else fetched from CCXT balance) |
| `EXECUTE_VWAP_SIZING` | Depth-aware qty clip before execute (default on; set `0` to disable) |
| `EXECUTE_BUDGET_CHECK` | Pre-trade notional lock across concurrent executes (default on; set `0` to disable) |
| `EXECUTE_VWAP_MAX_SPREAD_BPS` | Max book depth spread for VWAP clip (default `25`) |
| `HARDGATE_STATE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` / `HARDGATE_STATE_PASSPHRASE` | AES-256-GCM encrypt daemon state at rest (32-byte key or passphrase-derived) |
| `HARDGATE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` | HMAC-SHA256 secret for signed webhook payloads |
| `HARDGATE_WEBHOOK_SECRET_PREVIOUS` | Previous webhook secret during key rotation (both accepted) |
| `WORLDMONITOR_API_KEY`, `WORLDMONITOR_API_URL` | Optional [World Monitor](https://github.com/koala73/worldmonitor) API desk at `/api/worldmonitor/desk` (local fallback: F&G, Yahoo macro, FRED stress, Hyperliquid) |
| `HARDGATE_AGENT_SWARM` | Daemon (`app.js`) headless workforce each cycle — default **ON** when `TELEGRAM_*` set; set `0` to disable |
| `HARDGATE_AGENT_WATCH_MS` | Server **agent-watch** interval ms (default 5 min) — 24/7 swarm + Atomic scan + Telegram |
| `HARDGATE_AGENT_ALERT_MIN_SCORE` | Minimum quality score for AI AGENT Telegram push (default 35) |
| `/api/coindcx/*` | Cached server-side CoinDCX desk (instruments, marks, candles) — avoids public CORS proxy rate limits on Render |
| `ATOMIC_SCAN_TOP` | Top N contracts per venue for **Atomic Agents** Delta+CoinDCX pipeline (default 18) |
| `HARDGATE_FQS_GATE`, `HARDGATE_EDGE_GATE`, `HARDGATE_FT_EDGE_GATE` | Daemon formation quality gates (see Render worker section) |
| `/api/trading-stack/status` | Unified CCXT + OpenBB + Freqtrade + XM + execute status (see README Trading stack) |
| `XM_MT5_URL`, `XM_MT5_TOKEN`, `XM_GOLD_SYMBOL` | XM / MT5 gold candle bridge (`/api/xm/candles`) — same URL used for OMNIGOLD orders |
| `XM_OMNIGOLD_LIVE=1` | Send OMNIGOLD **TICKET** rows as gold lots to the XM bridge (default dry-run). Requires `HARDGATE_API_SECRET`. Crypto execute stays disabled. |
| `XM_OMNIGOLD_LOTS`, `XM_OMNIGOLD_MAX_LOTS` | Default / cap XM lot size (default `0.01` / `0.10`) |
| `XM_OMNIGOLD_BOT=0` | Disable the OMNIGOLD XM order API even for dry-run |
| OMNIGOLD **BACKTEST BOT** | In-browser replay of the XM send path on bars the scan fetched (TICKET + pending fill + stop-first, GROSS vs NET). Does not POST to XM. |

### Render daemon worker

`render.yaml` defines a **background worker** `hardgate-daemon` (`node app.js`). After blueprint sync:

1. Set `EXECUTE_CCXT_EXCHANGE`, `EXECUTE_CCXT_API_KEY`, `EXECUTE_CCXT_SECRET` on the **worker** service.
2. Set `HARDGATE_DAEMON_DRY_RUN=0` when ready for live orders (defaults to `1`).
3. Worker `buildCommand` installs Puppeteer + Chrome for headless BRAIN scans against `HARDGATE_URL`.
4. On conviction invalidation (EXPIRED / STOPPED / MOMENTUM DECAY), the daemon calls CCXT `cancelOrder` or `closePosition` via `lib/daemon-unwind.mjs` when not in dry run.
5. Optional Render **persistent disk** mount at `HARDGATE_STATE_FILE` path so `hardgate-daemon-state.json` survives redeploys.

Optional single-process dev: `HARDGATE_DAEMON_AUTOSTART=1` forks `app.js` from `scripts/server.mjs`.

| Variable | Purpose |
|----------|---------|
| `EXECUTE_FILL_POLL_URL` | Broker fill polling endpoint |
| `BOOK_EXECUTE_FILL_SECRET` | Webhook auth for fill updates |
| `BOOK_MAX_DAILY_LOSS_PCT` | Daily loss halt threshold (default 2%) |
| `FRED_API_KEY` | `/api/fred` macro series (DGS10, DTWEXBGS, DFII10, WALCL, WTREGEN, RRPONTSYD for REGIME fed-liquidity gauge) |
| `COINALYZE_API_KEY` | `/api/coinalyze` aggregated cross-venue OI (SMART $, OI FLOW, SQUEEZE) — free tier covers top ~50 perps |
| `COINGLASS_API_KEY` | `/api/coinglass` liquidation clusters (LIQUIDITY TRAP, TRADE PLAN SL check, SMC/OB confluence) — free tier = 4h refresh on majors |
| `TRADEOS_ACCESS_TOKEN` | TradeOS MCP OAuth bearer for `/api/tradeos/*` (run `npx -y @tradeos/tradeos-mcp oauth`) |
| `HARDGATE_FT_EDGE_GATE` | Enable Freqtrade expectancy gate on daemon formation (default off) |
| `HARDGATE_FT_PROTECT` | Enable Freqtrade cooldown + stoploss guard on formation (default off) |
| `HARDGATE_FT_MIN_EXPECTANCY` | Minimum Freqtrade expectancy to pass edge gate (default `0`) |
| `OPENBB_API_URL` | Optional self-hosted OpenBB REST backend (e.g. `http://127.0.0.1:6900`) |
| `OPENBB_API_USERNAME` / `OPENBB_API_PASSWORD` | Basic auth for OpenBB backend |
| `OPENBB_API_KEY` | Optional API key header for OpenBB backend |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY` | Chart vision (`/api/chart-vision/analyze`) — Gemini multimodal; heuristic fallback when unset. **Set on Render `hardgate-main` → Environment** (persists across deploys) or run `RENDER_API_KEY=… GEMINI_API_KEY=… node scripts/set-render-gemini-env.mjs` |
| `GEMINI_MODEL` | Gemini model for chart vision (default `gemini-3-flash-preview`; production Render sets this explicitly) |
| `CHART_VISION_VETO` | **On by default.** Set `0` / `false` to disable high-confidence vision conflict demotes |
| `CHART_VISION_PNG` | Set `0` / `false` to skip Puppeteer PNG render (Gemini falls back to SVG text) |
| `TELEGRAM_TOKEN`, `TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID` | Alert pushes (also overridable in browser localStorage). Update Render: `RENDER_API_KEY=… TELEGRAM_TOKEN=… node scripts/set-render-telegram-env.mjs` |
| `GH_DISPATCH_TOKEN` | GitHub Actions workflow dispatch from Render |
| `RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL` / `SELF_PING_URL` | Render keep-alive ping |

### E2E verification

1. Start `npm start`
2. Open `http://localhost:10000` — header should show HARDGATE branding and tab nav
3. Confirm `/api/proxy` returns 200: `curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" "http://localhost:10000/api/proxy?url=https://api.coindcx.com/exchange/v1/markets_details"`

Browser tabs load even when Binance/Delta REST is geo-blocked in the VM; CoinDCX/Yahoo proxy paths work server-side.

### Gotchas

- **Dual venue scan (default):** Opening a crypto scan tab auto-runs **Delta India + CoinDCX** (`hg_dual_scan` localStorage `0` disables). Header exchange toggle still sets desk context for TRADE PLAN / bias chips.
- **Binance geo-blocking (HTTP 451):** Cloud VMs may not reach `fapi.binance.com`. `npm test` passes offline. `tests/test-data-layer.mjs` skips Binance legs on 451 and exits 0.
- **Use the Node server, not `file://`:** Direct file open skips `/api/proxy`.
- **`npm install`:** Installs `ccxt`, `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk`, `puppeteer`, and optional `@resvg/resvg-js`. Re-run after pull if imports fail.
- **Stale dev server on `:10000`:** If `/api/chart-vision/capabilities` returns `not found`, kill the old `node scripts/server.mjs` process and restart (`npm start` in tmux) — an older instance may still be bound to the port.
- **Node 18+ required**
- **EDGE tab:** Cards show **FRESH / ACTIVE / STALE** from `barAge` (closed 4H bars since trigger). **FORMING NOW** lists bias-aligned symbols with **×ATR distance to EMA21**; Telegram via unified **15-min** `tabalerts.js` batch (EDGE + EDGE FORMING watch rows). `scripts/edge-diagnose.mjs` uses `HARDGATE_SITE` for `/api/proxy` when run from Node.
- **PINE tab:** Background `pineWarm` runs every **15 min** with the alert cycle (after `edgeWarm` for universe). Telegram for NEW/RECENT signals via unified **tabalerts** batch (`collectPine*`); manual PINE scan still uses `pineFireAlerts` directly. Requires **Alerts ON** and Telegram configured (`sendTelegram` / `TELEGRAM_*` env). **`tests/test-pine-tab.mjs`** smoke-tests `mount` / `refresh` / `pineWarm`, empty vs gated scan, and `HG_tabs` registration; **`tests/test-pine-scan.mjs`** covers signal eval/render math.
- **GOLD PINE tab:** Displays the **top 2 highest-probability formations** per SWING (4H) and SCALP (15m) section (`topProbSetups` in `goldpine.js`); full scored universe remains in `goldPineScan()` for alerts.
- **Gold venue (hg-v169):** `getXAUCandles` prefers **Delta XAUTUSD** first; proxy fallback stamps **PROXY** + `S.goldBasisPct`. **`tests/test-gold-venue.mjs`**.
- **Scorecard fill gate (hg-v172):** `hgScoreWalk` requires price to trade to the limit (12-bar window, matches LOG); **UNFILLED** / **PENDING_FILL** states; **NET expectancy** via `hgScoreNetR` reading `index.html`'s `hgCostR`. Hint boost weights net when available. **`tests/test-scorecard-fill.mjs`**.
- **Fix Pack 7 (hg-v173):** No baked Telegram defaults — `TELEGRAM_TOKEN` + `TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID` env / browser Settings only; **`tests/test-no-baked-secrets.mjs`**. **`hgGoldBasisNote()`** on gold TRADE PLAN, GOLD setup/deep, backtest subtitles when proxy feed. Scorecard **T1S** uses recorded **`scalePct`** (TRADE PLAN `tScale`, default 50%). Live-trading-disabled copy on TRADE PLAN + Settings.
- **Productivity (hg-v174):** BOOK **Lots** column via `bookContractsCell` + `hgQtyToContracts`; **FUNDING FADE** section on SWING/SCALP scans (`renderFadeSetupCard`); **`S.posSnapCache`** for SMART $ positioning (420s TTL); Binance **418/429 backoff** pauses scans + `brainAlertWarm`. **`tests/test-productivity-pack8.mjs`**.
- **Fix Pack 9 (hg-v175):** **`hgFundingCostR`** in net R for LOG + scorecard; SCORECARD Wilson 95% CI on win rate. **`tests/test-fix-pack-9.mjs`**.
- **Fix Pack 10 (hg-v176):** **Conviction mesh** — MR oppose → ASIDE; **bybitpos** BRAIN layer + BEST F8 cross boost; **FRED** macro on FTS stack; setup cards show agree/oppose/dark/silent counts. **`tests/test-fix-pack-10.mjs`**.
- **Fix Pack 11 (hg-v177+):** **Vol mesh** (DVOL, spot-perp, stables) + **RS F10** — `hgRelStrength` 30×4H vs BTC; BEST `famMax` scales denominator. **`tests/test-fix-pack-11.mjs`**, **`tests/test-relative-strength.mjs`**.

### Strategy → book plugin checklist

1. **Plan shape** — `entry`, `stop`, `t1`, optional `t2`
2. **CTA** — `bookBtnHTML(..., { scanner, strategy, layers, stack })` + `hgToTradePlanOnclickAttr` (or `hgSetupPanelHTML` / `carryTradeBtn` pattern)
3. **IN BOOK** — `hgBookStampChip(sym, dir, meta)` on card/panel headers (repaints via `hgBookStampRepaintDom` after tab switch / ADD TO BOOK)
4. **Routing** — `lib/book-routing.mjs` + `tests/test-book-routing.mjs`
5. **Tests** — `tests/test-<tab>.mjs`, `tests/test-handoff-coverage.mjs`; run `npm test`
6. **Ship** — bump cache in `sw.js` / script `?v=` when needed

### Gold shorts shown as HELD (hg-v593)
- OMNIGOLD card grid paints opposite-side tickets (shorts on an UP tape) as full `setupCard`s stamped **AGAINST GOLD TAPE** — not a dim queue.
- Shared uniform card (`hgGoldUniformCompose` / `hgGoldUniformHtml`) keeps the tape-aligned plan as **CONFIRMED COMBINED SETUP** and lists the best opposite-side A/B plan as **HELD · NOT CONFIRMED** with ENTRY / STOP / T1.
- GOLD SCALP / GOLD SWING tape-align **MOST PROBABLE** (`hgGoldUniformAlignedBest`) and stamp opposite cards **AGAINST GOLD TAPE · HELD**. Against-tape shorts are never MOST PROBABLE / SETUP ACTIVATED / CONFIRMED COMBINED.
- Tests: `tests/test-gold-uniform.mjs`, `tests/test-omnigold-opposite-shown.mjs`.

### Gold tabs (GOLD SCALP / GOLD SWING) — hg-v266 / hg-v550
- Both tabs use `hgApplyGoldBestLevels` + `hgGoldPostApplyRefresh` after `goldRankSetups` to snap entry/stop/TPs and re-sync **grade from tally** (A≥8, B≥5, else C) plus structural R:R tally legs.
- **Scalp** min R:R 1.2 at build; **swing** build gate stays 1.2 on the 1.5R ladder, and formation enforces **1.5R** via `HG_GOLD_SWING_MIN_RR` (`gold-best-levels.js`), which `tests/test-gold-swing-live-scope.mjs` asserts against that source. *(What this line used to say, and why it mattered, is in the changelog entry below — deliberately kept off this line so the normative statement carries exactly one number.)*
- Pass `candleSource` from `gold.src['15m']` (scalp) into the setup bundle: the **scalp** mint reads it for volume trust (a named paxg / xaut feed withholds the squeeze and MFI votes). GOLD SWING sets `microOpts.candleSource` from `gold.src['4h']` too, and **the swing mint reads it nowhere** — hg-v976 measured the swing board byte-identical with and without it and asserts the field unread, so a later pack that wires a swing volume-trust rule must say so and measure it (the hg-v966 trap); the mixed-feed A+ banner reads `gold.mixed`, not this field.
- Swing tab mirrors scalp: GOLD A+ panel, `hgTallyLegAudit` chips, mixed-feed banner when `gold.mixed`.
- **Institutional filters (`hgGoldInstFilter` in `goldind.js`):** sweep setups need MSS + displacement + IFVG/FVG on the execution TF (15m scalp / 4h swing) — a liquidity grab alone is rejected (`SWEEP BLOCK`). OB displacement volume must exceed the prior 5-bar average (`OB TRAP`). Gold **longs** are killed when DXY **and** TNX are bullish (EMA50 / `RISING`) — missing feeds fail-open (`CONVICTION LOCK` here is a signal kill, not `conviction-lock.js`). Asia **00:00–08:00 UTC** demotes/rejects standard scalp execution unless Asian-range or a violent AH/AL sweep; swing **demotes**. London 08:00 GMT and NY overlap 12:00–16:00 GMT get session priority weight. **News-gate:** CPI / NFP / FOMC / GDP lock **new** scalp and swing minting **30 min before / 15 min after**; other high-impact still NEWS-FADE. Live booked convictions keep running. **Spread lock:** live bid/ask wider than **250 points / 2.5 pips ($0.25)** kills the entry; missing quotes fail-open. **MTF matrix:** scalp longs need H4 **and** Daily `price > EMA20 > EMA50`; HTF conflict locks scalp and leaves Gold Wing open; missing HTF fail-open. **1.5×ATR14 is a FLOOR, never a cap** (structure may widen to and beyond 3.5× scalp / 4.0× swing).
- **OMNIGOLD native path (hg-v551):** the same `hgGoldInstFilter` stack runs as one hard `inst-filter` ledger row on detector tickets (`hgOgGates` / `hgOgEvaluate`). ENTRY stays `hit.level`. goldind absent fail-opens. GOLD SCALP/SWING engine bridge already filtered in `goldScalpSetups` / `goldSwingSetups`. Do **not** send native tickets through `hgApplyGoldBestLevels`. Tests: `tests/test-omnigold-inst-gates.mjs`.
- **Gold desks empty fix (hg-v552):** OMNIGOLD paints MOST PROBABLE + a loading engines strip **before** the gold-tab bridge fetch (8s timeout → `ok:false` why, never blank `#ogMp` / `#ogGoldEngines`). GOLD SCALP/SWING keep last cards while rescanning. Asia on `goldScalpSetups` **demotes** (`hardReject:false`) so demoted cards still paint; OMNIGOLD native tickets keep `sessionHard` Asia hard-reject. Auto-scan `mustScan` includes `omnigold` / `goldscalp` / `goldswing`. Tests: `tests/test-gold-desks-empty-fix.mjs`.
- **GOLD SCALP populate (hg-v572):** MTF bias / conflict on the scalp `hardReject:false` path **demotes** (stamp `MTF BIAS` / `MTF CONFLICT`) instead of hard-dropping into QUALITY GATES only — demoted cards still populate and cannot lead. OMNIGOLD hard path unchanged. Part5–7 SCALP mint fixed to read `inp` (was undeclared `opts`). Tests: `tests/test-gold-scalp-populate.mjs`.
### The ROTATION season reached one of its four consumers: STAR TRADER compared against strings the signal never emits, the FTS stack and AI AGENT read fields the snapshot never carried — hg-v994
- **`rotation.js` snapshots `season: 'alt' | 'btc' | 'mixed'` and the alt share.** BRAIN reads it correctly and always did. The other three consumers did not. **STAR TRADER** fed the *snapshot* back into `rotationSignal` — a classifier that wants the raw top-50 market list, so it answered `mixed` on every call — and then compared the result against **`'altseason'` / `'btcseason'`**, strings the signal has never emitted. Two independent defects, either sufficient: the **ROTATION vote had never fired**, and since hg-v991 its `vote:ROTATION` read mark was **always absent**. **The FTS setup stack** read **`rot.btcSeason`**, a field the snapshot never carried, so its *BTC season is a headwind for a long* caution had **never fired on any desk**. **AI AGENT**'s Market Analyst read **`rot.leader`**, also never carried, so its rotation finding never fired. Four readers, one snapshot, three dead — found by reading which field each consumer asks for against the shape the writer produces, the check hg-v985 applied to OI FLOW's `W`/`G` alias.
- **Two directional reads, one home each, in `rotation.js`** — and they are BRAIN's reading, stated once: **`hgRotationAgainst(season, dir, sym)`** — BTC season is a headwind for an **alt** long (`true`); never for a BTC long, because BTC is what leads it; a short, alt season or a mixed read is not against (`false`); an unknown season or no direction is **no verdict** (`undefined` — and `'altseason'` is an unknown season). **`hgRotationFavours(season, dir, sym)`** — alt season favours an alt long, BTC season a BTC long; **longs only**, because this layer has only ever voted a long. The BTC test reads the contract under its spellings (`BTCUSDT`, `BTC-PERP`, `BTCUSD`, `BTC`).
- **The three consumers read the season the snapshot carries, through the home.** STAR TRADER casts its `+1` through `hgRotationFavours` on `ctx.rotation.season` — driven through the real `stContextVotes`: alt season votes an alt long and not a BTC long, BTC season votes a BTC long and **not** an alt long (no against-vote is invented: this desk never had one), a mixed read and the old `'altseason'` string vote nothing. The setup stack cautions through `hgRotationAgainst` and stays **neutral** otherwise (no `+1` is invented there either); a null alt share prints no share, never `0%`; gold takes no rotation item. AI AGENT reports the season as a **note** in its summary and pushes **no finding** from it — a `leader` finding was never measured and is not made live by being reachable. **With the home absent each consumer casts or bumps nothing** rather than carrying a second rule.
- **The forward ledger records three fields on every record** at `hgFwdRecordScan` — `rotSeason`, `rotAltPct`, `rotAgainst` — through **`hgRotationMark(dir, sym)`** on the live snapshot (a gold-lane symbol gets nothing; a string alt share or stamp is never coerced), the hg-v984 shape: a valid desk hand-in wins field by field (a partial hand-in keeps what it named and takes the rest from the shared read), junk (`'BTC'`, `'20'`, `'yes'`) is ignored, `hgFwdNormalize` refuses the same junk through the direct door, an alt share of exactly zero is a READ zero. `hgFwdFold` carries **`rt[season]`**, **`rta` / `rto`**; **`hgFwdRotationSplit`** prints **ROTATION SEASON SPLIT** (cautioned vs not, per-season cells, unmarked as NEITHER) on the shared forward panel and **nothing** where no record carries a mark.
- **What changes on a board, said plainly:** a vote and a caution written long ago and never fired now fire on the season that exists. The STAR TRADER vote is `+1` toward a tier; the FTS caution is `−1` on the fundamental pillar. **No threshold moves, no gate is loosened, no setup is removed from any board by this pack**, and the marks gate nothing (asserted over eleven files).
- **The mutation pass found three fixture gaps in this pack's own guard:** the AI AGENT junk-season case was never exercised (a `leader`-only snapshot has no season at all, so *any season string* and *a known season* read alike); the junk `'yes'` hand-in sat on a record the shared read would also have called against; and the desk-wins rule was only ever driven on a hand-in that named all three fields, so the shared read never ran beside it. Each fixture now hands in what the shared read would **not** say.
- Tests: `tests/test-rotation-season-marks.mjs` (63 assertions; **44/44** behavioural mutations caught across `rotation.js`, `startradertab.js`, `setup-stack.js`, `ai-agent.js` and `hg-forward.js`, baseline verified green before every pass).

### The REGIME layer runs two hard rules, on eight desks between them, and neither had ever been recorded beside an outcome; the PINE hard block carried its own copy of one — hg-v993
- **Two rules, both hard, both unmeasured.** (1) The **playbook bias** (`regimeState().playbook.bias`: LONG-ONLY / SHORT-ONLY / BOTH / STAND-ASIDE) is a **hard block on PINE** (`pineRegimeAllows`), an `against` on the FTS setup stack, a **+2 vote on STAR TRADER** and a BRAIN layer. (2) The **per-symbol tape regime** (`detectRegime` on the series in hand: VOLATILE kills trend continuation, COMPRESSION kills swing) is a **hard veto** through `hgRegimeAllowsSetup` on **SWING, SCALP, BEST, SUPER SETUP, CHART VISION, CONTRACT REPORT** and the FTS stack — and **asked by none of the crypto desks that record most** (OMNIROUTE, OMNIPRESENT, SQUEEZE, OI FLOW, DEX SCREENER, CRYPTOVERSE, 90PERCENT). No forward record carried the bias, either verdict, the tape key, or the snapshot's **age** — `regimeState().at` was read by **nothing**, so a snapshot hours old is applied as current on every desk. STAR TRADER's hg-v991 `vote:REGIME` mark is the one trace, and it collapses STAND-ASIDE and BOTH into a single `false`. The hg-v984 / v985 / v992 shape, on the layer named in the standing list.
- **One rule, one home, and the PINE gate reads it.** `pinegate.js` carried its **own copy** of the bias table (`=== 'STAND-ASIDE'`, `=== 'LONG-ONLY' && dir === 'short'`, …). It lives once now — **`hgRegimeBiasBlocks(bias, dir)`** in `regime.js`: `true` (STAND-ASIDE, or an ONLY bias on the other side), `false` (the ONLY side, or BOTH), `undefined` (unknown bias, or no direction — no direction is no verdict *even under STAND-ASIDE*). `pineRegimeAllows` **delegates** to it and, with the home absent, **fails open and says so** rather than carrying a second table; driven through the real `pineGateIntersect`, PINE blocks **exactly** what the rule says under all four biases and produces the counts it always did (STAND-ASIDE 2, LONG-ONLY 1, BOTH 0). `tests/test-pinegate.mjs` boots `regime.js` first, as `index.html` loads them — a real coupling introduced on purpose, not a harness convenience. **`hgRegimeBiasMark(dir, sym)`** reads the live snapshot for a record at fire time: the bias, the verdict, and **`ageMin`** (whole minutes; an epoch-zero or string stamp is not an age, never coerced); a **gold-lane symbol gets no verdict**, because this is a crypto regime.
- **`hgTapeRegimeMark(rows, style)`** in `plans.js`, beside the gate: the `detectRegime` key on the series and the veto the gate **in force for that style** would have applied — **equals `hgRegimeAllowsSetup(...).allow === false` on 25 of 25 (tape × style) pairs**, on synthetic tapes the guard first proves reach **every** key (volatile, compression, trend, range, weak_trend). VOLATILE vetoes swing and scalp and not best; COMPRESSION vetoes swing, not scalp, and is friendly to mean reversion — the gate's own asymmetries, read rather than restated. A series under 60 bars: the gate **fails open**, the mark says **NOT RECORDED** — allowed is the wrong word for unread.
- **The forward ledger records five fields on every record** at `hgFwdRecordScan`: `regimeBias`, `regimeAgainst`, `regimeAgeMin`, `tapeRegime`, `tapeVeto`. The tape is read off the **first candle series the candidate carries under the names the writers already use** (`rows`, `rows4h`, `rows1h`; at least 60 bars) and judged under the style the record's **timeframe** implies — scalp for the intraday frames, swing otherwise — so **compression on a 15m record reads allowed and on a 4h record reads vetoed**, as the two gates do; a candidate carrying no series records NOT RECORDED. A valid desk hand-in wins over the shared read on all five; junk (`CHOP`, `1`, `long-only`, `yes`, `-5`) is ignored and the shared read decides, **never coerced**; `hgFwdNormalize` refuses the same junk through the **direct door**; an age of exactly zero is a READ zero. `hgFwdFold` carries **`ra` / `ro`** (playbook), **`tp[key]`** and **`tv` / `tw`** (tape) so both splits outlive the live cap. **`hgFwdRegimeSplit`** / **`hgFwdTapeRegimeSplit`** read settled records (per-bias and per-tape cells, the **median snapshot age at fire**) and print **REGIME PLAYBOOK SPLIT** and **TAPE REGIME SPLIT** on the shared forward panel — **nothing** where no record carries a mark.
- **Driven end to end:** nine records under a dark snapshot and a LONG-ONLY snapshot 30 minutes old, on volatile / trend / compression / thin tapes across `rows`, `rows4h`, `rows1h`, on 4h and 15m records, settled on hand-dated bars: `3 stood down · 5 allowed · 1 NEITHER`, `4 vetoed · 3 allowed · 2 NEITHER`, median age 30 min, the gold record carrying the tape and no playbook verdict, the open record folding nothing.
- **Nothing is gated on the marks:** no gate module or desk reads them (asserted over nine files), the gate itself does not read the mark, the ledger reads each mark through one guard-and-call pair, **no threshold moves, no setup leaves any board**. What changes is that the two REGIME rules can now be asked, out of sample, whether they separate — on the desks that apply them and on the desks that never did.
- **The mutation pass found four fixture gaps and one duplicated check in this pack's own work:** the desk hand-ins (a stood-down verdict, a coerced `'yes'`) agreed with the shared read on the first fixture, so ignoring or coercing them was invisible — the fixtures now hand in what the shared read would *not* say; the stood-down cohort summed to exactly **0.000R**, so dropping the sum was invisible — the fixture moved; a candidate with one series could not tell *the first array* from *the first readable one* — a thin `rows` beside a readable `rows4h` can; and `hgTapeRegimeMark` repeated `detectRegime`'s own 60-bar guard, which made the whitelist unreachable for `'unknown'` and the guard an unkillable mutant — the duplicate is gone and the thin rule has one home. One mutation pattern matched the **sentiment** split's identical line first and never mutated the tape split; it names its function now.
- Tests: `tests/test-regime-tape-marks.mjs` (67 assertions; **53/53** behavioural mutations caught across `regime.js`, `pinegate.js`, `plans.js` and `hg-forward.js`, baseline verified green before every pass); `tests/test-pinegate.mjs` boots the one home.

### The news calendar read CLEAR when it had not loaded, on the one gate and the one vote that ask it; and neither the blackout nor the Fear & Greed guard was recorded beside an outcome anywhere — hg-v992
- **`hgNewsRisk` answered `risk: 'low', blackout: false` on a cold cache and on a calendar leg that failed while the F&G leg loaded, and nothing in the shape said so.** Both are honest dark states; both read as a clearance. **GATES G5** (`engine.js`) read that as `newsClear = true` — its own *not loaded* branch tested whether the function existed, which it always does. **STAR TRADER** cast a **+1 calendar clear** vote on it (`stContextVotes`) and, since hg-v991, wrote a **`vote:NEWS` read mark of `true`** into its forward record from it — a mark the ledger would later have counted as "the calendar agreed". **`hgNewsGate`** on OMNIROUTE / OMNIGOLD told a not-loaded calendar apart from a clear one only by **matching the note text** (`/not loaded|news error/`), and the calendar-failed case carried no such note. Found while reading which of the eight desks on the standing list feed a rule anything measures: NEWS feeds three, and none of them could tell *clear* from *unread*.
- **`hgNewsRisk` carries `unchecked: true` in the three dark cases** — cold, calendar leg failed (with a note that names it), thrown — and the risk fields keep their old values for callers that only know the old shape. **G5 reads the flag** and prints `news calendar not loaded — blackout unverified; check the calendar manually` as its NA reason (the same reason it already printed when the function was absent); **`hgNewsGate` reads the flag** and still the note for older shapes; **STAR TRADER casts no NEWS vote on an unchecked calendar**, so the read mark is **absent** (hg-v989's third state), never fabricated. Driven on the real modules: cold and calendar-failed both read G5 NA and no STAR TRADER vote; a loaded calendar with no event in the window still reads clear and still votes.
- **Two marks, one home each, in `news.js` beside the reader they belong to.** **`hgNewsMark(sym)`** is the calendar class in force — `blackout` / `high` / `med` / `low` — or `undefined` when unchecked. **`hgFngExtremeMark(value, dir, sym)`** is the **BIAS S2 guard's** verdict (F&G **≥ 80** blocks a fresh long, **≤ 20** a fresh short): `true` VETOED, `false` allowed, `undefined` on a non-number (`'55'` is not a read, `+null` is not zero), a missing direction, or a **gold-lane symbol** the crypto index does not speak for. The bar is **`HG_FNG_GUARD`**, the shell's `runBias` S2 guard is pinned to the same two numbers, and **`hgOmniMarketSide` stands aside exactly where the mark says VETOED on 10 F&G values across a long tape** — one rule, proved equal, not restated. STAR TRADER's SENTIMENT *vote* uses **75 / 25**, a different bar in its own file; it is named and left as it is.
- **The forward ledger records all three on every record** at `hgFwdRecordScan` — `newsRisk`, `fng`, `fngVeto` — through the hg-v984 shape: a desk that hands a **valid** value in wins (a desk verdict that *disagrees* with the shared read wins too, asserted), junk (`'LOW'`, `'55'`, `1`) is ignored and the shared read decides, never coerced; **`hgFwdNormalize`** refuses the same junk through the **direct door** (`hgFwdRecord`), so the rule holds with no scan helper in front of it; a record fired with the module cold reads NOT RECORDED on all three. `hgFwdFold` carries **`nw[class]`, `fv`, `fo`** buckets so the split outlives the live cap. **`hgFwdNewsSplit` / `hgFwdSentimentSplit`** read settled records and print **NEWS CALENDAR SPLIT** (per class: n, hit, R) and **SENTIMENT GUARD SPLIT** (vetoed vs allowed, unmarked as NEITHER, folded tails) on the shared forward panel — and **nothing** where no record carries a mark. Driven end to end: nine records under cold / calendar-failed / blackout / high / low seeds, settled on hand-dated bars, split into `blackout 2 · high 1 · low 2 · 3 NEITHER` and `2 vetoed · 5 allowed · 1 NEITHER`, the XAUUSD record carrying the value and **no** verdict.
- **Nothing is gated on the marks:** no gate module reads them (asserted), no threshold moves, no setup leaves any board. G5 and the STAR TRADER vote only **stop claiming a clearance they never checked** — a setup that formed under an unread calendar reads NA / no vote, which is what it always was.
- Tests: `tests/test-news-sentiment-marks.mjs` (55 assertions; **36/36** behavioural mutations caught across `news.js`, `hg-gates.js`, `engine.js`, `startradertab.js` and `hg-forward.js`, baseline verified green before every pass); `tests/test-news.mjs` follows the flag.

### STAR TRADER crowned PRIME and HIGH, printed ADD TO BOOK and SEND TO TRADE PLAN, and wrote no forward record of any kind — hg-v991
- **The desk whose whole thesis is multi-family confluence could never be judged.** `startradertab.js` carried **no reference to hg-forward at all**: it synthesizes SWING, SCALP, EDGE, SQUEEZE and MEAN REV votes with **NEWS, SENTIMENT (F&G), REGIME, ROTATION, ON-CHAIN, SMART $, OI FLOW, STRUCTURE and the CONTEXT GATES**, crowns PRIME and HIGH, pins MOST PROBABLE, feeds ChartVision and hands the plan to the book — and recorded none of it. The hg-v981 census of **28** crypto writers was derived from the files that call the ledger, so a desk that never called it was invisible to that census. None of the context layers on this desk (the NEWS, BIAS, REGIME, ROTATION and GATES tabs, as votes) had ever been asked here whether it separates.
- **Every crypto synthesis with a plan is recorded now** — PRIME, HIGH and WATCH drafts alike, so the pool measures the raw synthesis and **`ticket`** marks a crowned non-draft — under mechanic **`ST-<plan family>`** (SWING, SCALP, EDGE, SMART, NEAR: which vote supplied the plan, now carried on the synthesis as `planFamily`), dated on the **last CLOSED 4h bar** the desk read (never the clock; `stDropForming` runs at the fetch site), carrying the **mark**, the **venue funding** the ticker had (the ledger derives the hg-v985 verdict), and one **hg-v989 read mark per vote FAMILY**: `true` when the family agreed with points, `false` when it voted against or only cautioned (a NEWS caution is a calendar that was not clear), **absent** when the family cast no vote — silent or unloaded alike, which the record cannot tell apart and does not pretend to. The vote-family universe is read off the source and fits under the ledger's 16-read cap, so no mark is silently dropped. Open records **settle on the 4h bars each scan fetches, before that bar's syntheses are recorded**; the **shared forward panel** is painted under the cards; the stat line counts records written.
- **Driven through the real tab** (mount, click, wait for the stat line): two crypto records dated on the closed bar with mark, funding and the SWING / SCALP / REGIME / NEWS / SENTIMENT marks; a second scan on the same bar writes **nothing** (the ledger dedups on the bar); the next closed bar trading through T1 **settles both**, and `hgFwdReadSplit` can ask whether REGIME separates on this desk — for the first time.
- **What is deliberately NOT recorded, and why:** the gold lane and the non-crypto contracts. An FX or index symbol would be read by the ledger's crypto macro mark (hg-v984) as an alt long; a gold row would have to enter the three gold-writer censuses and carry its feed (hg-v979) — a pack of its own. The guard asserts both record nothing, so a later pack knows it was a choice.
- **Two writer censuses misread a ternary as a record key, found by the new writer:** `tests/test-crypto-funding-mark.mjs` and `tests/test-crypto-ledger-fill-mark.mjs` locate a record literal by the last `entry:` in a window after the row builder — and `p.entry : null`, a ternary in the card renderer 3,000 characters later, matched `\bentry\s*:`, sent the census to the wrong braces and judged the new writer **bare**. The key regex now refuses a preceding dot; every existing writer classifies as before.
- **Nothing is gated on any of it:** the synthesis reads nothing back from the ledger (asserted), no threshold moves, no setup leaves any board. What changes is that this desk's forward record exists.
- Tests: `tests/test-startrader-forward.mjs` (44 assertions; **16/16** behavioural mutations caught on `startradertab.js`, baseline verified green before every pass); the fill-mark, funding and macro censuses now count `startradertab.js`.

### CRYPTO ULTRA gated itself MEASURED NOT TRADABLE on a walk that measured nothing: every replay harness handed the engine a rule without geometry, every plan was priced at stop null and target null, and zero settled trades in every cell was typed up as an architectural verdict — hg-v990
- **The committed artifact** (`scripts/backtest-cryptoultra-results.json`, 2026-08-22 → 2026-09-12, 1,999 × 15m BTCUSDT bars) has **14 grid cells**, fires **1,628** times at its loosest cell, settles **ZERO** trades in every cell, and carries `chosen: null` and `evidence: null`. The hand-typed `HG_CRYPTO_ULTRA_EVIDENCE` in `cryptoultra.js` said **`measured: true`** and diagnosed *"architectural failure — the 470-indicator voting model does not work … long and short always fire on identical bars, canceling each other through merge logic"*, tested across BTC, ETH, SOL and 1h. `tests/test-cryptoultra.mjs` asserted that text. **No artifact in the repo ever produced it** — the hg-v946 / hg-v960 hand-typed-evidence failure, on the desk that calls itself VERIFIED.
- **The cause is a harness seam, reproduced on the real engine, not a property of the strategy.** Every replay (`backtest-cryptoultra.mjs`, `backtest-crypto-multisymbol.mjs`, `backtest-cryptoscan-improved.mjs`) passes `rule: { minPct: 0, minAvail: 0, regimeGate: false }`, and the engine read **`inp.rule || RULE`** — so that object *replaced* the rule: `stopAtr`, `costFloorMult`, `t1R`, `t2R` and `timeoutBars` were undefined, `rawStop` and the cost floor were NaN, and every plan the walk kept carried **stop null, target null**. A trade with no stop and no target never exits on price, times out with NaN R, and every aggregate silently dropped it (`isFinite(netR)`); **1,512 merged of 1,628 fired** is exactly what one un-exitable trade per side produces. **40 of 40** synthetic tapes priced a NaN stop under the override, **0 of 40** with the rule merged. The multi-symbol claim rests on the same seam.
- **The engine merges an override over RULE** — an override tunes the fields it names and inherits the rest (`minPct: 0.99` still gates; `stopAtr: 3.0` still moves the stop; the plan under the harness override now equals the plan under RULE level for level) — **and refuses a plan whose stop distance or target ladder is not a positive finite number** as a named gate with `plan: null`, never a plan whose stop is null. `stopAtr: null` reads a stop distance of **zero** (`+null` is 0, the trap again) and is refused; `t1R: null` is not a ladder and is refused; a key named as `undefined` is refused rather than inherited silently.
- **The walk refuses to report on plans it could not price:** `scanBars` counts **unpriced** plans (`READS.unpriced`, recorded in `meta`) and keeps them out of the trade walk; **`assertPriced`** is fatal before anything is written, naming the count and the seam (the hg-v921 rule); the walk's functions are exported and its main runs only when it is the entry point. **A second defect found by driving it:** the per-bar `READS = {…}` reassignment wiped the counter every bar — it mutates now. Driven on synthetic bars through the real engine, every kept plan has finite geometry, trades settle with finite net R and **every settled trade counts**; a stub engine pricing NaN stops is counted **30 of 30** unpriced and the write is refused.
- **The evidence literal writes itself** (`scripts/cryptoultra-evidence-literal.mjs`, markers, `--write`, missing marker fatal; `npm run cu:evidence` is the drift check): with no rule chosen it writes **`measured: false`** and a **note derived from the artifact** — *fired 1628 times at its loosest cell and settled 0 trades in every one of its 14 cells … a defect of the walk, not a verdict on the strategy* — and it says a different sentence for a walk that fired nothing, one that settled too few for the floor, and one that recorded its own unpriced count; with a chosen rule it writes `measured: true` from the artifact's own figures and verdict, `tradable` the boolean or false. **No hand-typed diagnosis survives anywhere.**
- **Nothing loosens.** NOT YET MEASURED is record-only exactly as MEASURED NOT TRADABLE was: no fire, no ticket; CRYPTO SCAN prints RECORD ONLY as before. **Nothing is re-baked here** (403 CONNECT on every route, no bar cache), so what ships is the seam, the refusal and an honest literal — the first walk on a machine that can fetch is the first measurement this desk will ever have had.
- **The full suite caught a defect in my own hg-v989 guard, not me:** `tests/test-forward-read-marks.mjs` pinned `version: 'hg-v989'` by hand, so the first suite run of this pack went red with nothing wrong — the shape `tests/helpers/build-version.mjs` exists to prevent. It reads the version now, as this pack's guard does.
- Tests: `tests/test-cryptoultra-evidence.mjs` (51 assertions; **20/20** behavioural mutations caught across `cryptoultra.js`, `scripts/backtest-cryptoultra.mjs` and `scripts/cryptoultra-evidence-literal.mjs`, baseline verified green before every pass); `tests/test-cryptoultra.mjs` follows NOT YET MEASURED.

### The replay named the reads and nothing recorded them going forward: every OMNIROUTE and OMNIPRESENT forward record marks the reads its own replay flagged, and the ledger counts them on settled records beside the replay row — hg-v989
- **hg-v987 measured every signal-time read OMNIROUTE scores by and named two leans — momentum convergence at or over half its max, and the live-fresh read — as *worth a forward measurement*; hg-v988 found one out-of-sample verdict on OMNIPRESENT (the stretched-from-EMA21 exhaustion read, WORSE 4/4) and wrote that *the forward ledger is where a confirmation would come from*. Neither desk's forward record carried any of those reads.** The ledger could confirm nothing: the replay named the reads and nothing recorded them going forward — the hg-v955 shape (*the work was done and dropped on the way out*), one pack later and in my own packs again.
- **One field, three states, booleans only (hg-v955 / hg-v984 / hg-v985):** a record carries **`reads`**, an object of named booleans — `true` (the record carried the read), `false` (it did not), absent (**NOT RECORDED**: a desk that never asked, or a record from before it asked). ONLY the two booleans are kept per read; `1`, `0`, `'no'`, `''`, `{}` and `null` are dropped, never coerced. Keys are bounded (48 characters, 16 per record) so a caller cannot turn the record store into a dumping ground. Each read **folds** into its own true/false pair in the aggregate so the split outlives the live cap, as every other mark does; **`hgFwdReadSplit`** counts the marked cohort against its complement on settled records with the unmarked counted as **NEITHER** (and the folded pairs beyond the cap read back per read), and **`hgFwdReadSplitHtml`** prints **REPLAY READ SPLIT** on the shared forward panel — and **nothing** while no record carries a mark, because an empty split is not a clean bill.
- **The set of reads is READ off each desk's literal, never typed:** the two generators now write **`tab`** into the literals, and OMNIROUTE's writes **`pillarHalf`** — half the replay max of every varying pillar, the bar the replay judged at — so the live desk marks the **same** read the replay measured rather than a threshold of its own; a re-bake that finds a new verdict marks it with no edit, and a read a desk has no reader for is left **absent, never guessed**. Both committed literals round-trip at **zero drift**.
- **OMNIPRESENT (`opReadMarks`)** marks every read in `verdicts ∪ leans` it has a reader for, with the **generator's own predicates**: an evidence family is a **prefix** of the evidence string (`hasEv`), a zone source is membership (`hasSrc`), the two hg-v421 hard gates are the counts they gate on, the distance bands read `zone.distAtr`. Proved on the replay itself: the desk mark equals the generator's pick on **3,006** sampled (row, read) pairs; **every verdict read is marked on every row**, so a verdict cannot be silently unmeasured; the four geometry bands have no reader and stay absent (derivable later from the record's own levels). Driven through the real scan, every record carries six reads and **agrees with `opReadMarks` of the TRIGGERED candidate by construction**; with `omniroute.js` absent the desk still marks — the marks need no renderer.
- **OMNIROUTE (`hgOmniReadMarks`):** the compact solidity stamp — the one scorer that graded the card — now carries **eighteen `[score, max]` pairs** and no detail strings, so a **TICKET** marks `pillar:<p>:half` against the replay half (**12 and 10** for the two marked pillars: the live max is exactly twice the replay half, the same bar live and replayed); a **non-ticket is stamped late, after recording, and carries NO pillar read** — absent, not false, said on the record rather than invented. `gate:liveFresh` and `grade:liveFresh` are **hg-solidity's own G2** (`hgSolGateLiveFresh`) on the plan and the decision bar's `livePx` — one rule; **absent** with `hg-solidity.js` absent rather than re-derived here, and absent when the gate says `no-live`, which is the gate saying it could not read the price, not a read of the plan. The two live-fresh reads **differ** on a pullback-side price (the gate passes as `pending`, the grade is not `fresh`), as the two replay rows do.
- **Replay beside forward, on one row, through the one renderer:** `hgOmniFactorSepHtml` prints a **forward (settled)** column beside every replay row on **both** desks (via `T.tab`) and a **FORWARD** line naming how many settled records carry marks; a read the desk does not mark shows an **empty cell**, never a fabricated count; nothing renders about the forward half until a marked record settles. **Nothing is gated on any of it:** no gate module reads the marks (asserted), no threshold moves, no setup leaves any board — a read that separates on the replay AND forward is still a measurement until someone decides otherwise, and the panel says so.
- Tests: `tests/test-forward-read-marks.mjs` (71 assertions; **30/30** behavioural mutations caught across `hg-forward.js`, `omnipresent.js` and `omniroute.js`, baseline verified green before every pass); `tests/test-omniroute-factor-separation.mjs` and `tests/test-omnipresent-factor-separation.mjs` follow the one new reader of each literal.

### One out-of-sample verdict on OMNIPRESENT, and it runs against the desk: the stretched-from-EMA21 exhaustion read, one of the families its own hard gate counts, is WORSE in all four disjoint windows; neither hard gate separates; and the method has one home for both crypto desks — hg-v988
- **hg-v987 asked which signal-time read OMNIROUTE scores by separates winners on its own replay. This pack asks OMNIPRESENT the same question, and first gives the question one home:** `scripts/factor-sep-core.mjs` holds the window rule (through hg-v984's `separate`, the hg-v920 rule), the lean / degenerate / in-sample classification, the literal writer and the marker splice; `scripts/omniroute-factor-separation.mjs` is rebuilt as a **config over the core** and round-trips the committed `HG_OMNI_FACTOR_SEP` at **zero drift**, so the second desk is a config, not a copy (hg-v949: a second copy of a rule is a second rule).
- **OMNIPRESENT tickets only a TRIGGERED zone that clears two HARD gates** — confluence ≥ 3 zone sources and ≥ 2 exhaustion reads (hg-v421, *"the tab already claimed this"*) — and its replay stamped both verdicts, the zone sources, the evidence read, the distance to the zone, the score and the cost on **8,502** walked trades (2026-06-13 → 2026-09-11). `scripts/omnipresent-factor-separation.mjs` judges **32 reads**: the two gates and the ticket population that clears both, confluence and evidence counts, the score against the book median, the distance bands, **every zone source and every exhaustion family the replay ever read** (derived from the rows — the `stretched ±N×ATR` strings collapse to **one** family), the cost ceiling in force (0.12R, hg-v607) and a 0.30R band, the stop bands, the side, and five UTC sessions that partition every row.
- **Out of sample: ONE verdict, and it runs against the desk.** The **stretched-from-EMA21 exhaustion read** (n=836) is **WORSE in all four windows on win, gross and net**: win **26.4%** against 31.0%, gross **−0.200R**, net **−0.369R** against −0.215R. It is one of the reads the evidence gate **counts toward its two** — and hg-v421's own trend-guard already says *"stretch is the trend"*. **Neither hard gate separates:** the confluence gate leans better on **net alone** (2/2/4, n=2,740, −0.145R vs −0.271R); the evidence gate is **worse in three of four on all three figures** (n=596, 28.5% vs 30.7%, −0.265R vs −0.228R) — not a verdict, not a lean, and not the direction the gate assumes; the **ticket population** that clears both (n=165) reads **25.5%** win at **−0.334R** against −0.228R, also 1/1/1. **Nine leans, named as leans:** the cost ceiling in force leans worse on net while **better on win and gross in three windows** (cost arithmetic, `costR = rtCost / stopPct`); **prior-day high** as a zone source leans better (3/4/4, gross **+0.023R**); a zone **≥ 1×ATR away** leans better on net while **worse on win in every window** — the stop-width arithmetic again; `value-area low` is worse on win and gross in all four and net in three (0/0/1), one figure short of a verdict, and is reported as exactly that.
- **A verdict here is a measurement, not a gate.** Nothing on OMNIPRESENT is wired to it: the `stretched` read still counts toward the evidence gate exactly as before, because a rule that removes 836 rows on one bake with no forward record is the hg-v966 trap even when the sign is unanimous — the finding is **published on the desk**, and the forward ledger (which records this desk's TRIGGERED head, hg-v981) is where a confirmation would come from. **One renderer for two desks:** OMNIPRESENT paints `HG_OP_FACTOR_SEP` through **`hgOmniFactorSepHtml`** (hg-v987) under its cards on **both** paint paths (the scan paint and the PAID view), and with `omniroute.js` absent it renders **nothing** rather than a second renderer; the literal is read by that call and by nothing else (asserted in code). `npm run op:factor-sep` is the drift check. **One fill bound**, said on the panel — and said to matter less here, because this desk enters at the live print (8,426 of 8,502 rows filled on bar one).
- **The suite caught a vacuous assertion in this pack's own guard, not me:** its first cut carried `ok(!R1.sorted || true, …)`, which cannot fail; `tests/test-suite-not-vacuous.mjs` sweeps for exactly that shape and went red. It asserts the sorted rows and that both desk generators drop them before the literal now.
- **No gate is loosened, no threshold moves, no setup leaves any board.**
- Tests: `tests/test-omnipresent-factor-separation.mjs` (40 assertions; **17/17** behavioural mutations caught across `scripts/factor-sep-core.mjs`, `scripts/omnipresent-factor-separation.mjs` and `omnipresent.js`, baseline verified green before every pass); `tests/test-omniroute-factor-separation.mjs` stays green over the shared core.

### None of the 36 signal-time reads OMNIROUTE scores by separates winners on its own 2,833-trade replay across four disjoint windows; the only unanimous separator is the module's own in-sample demotion, and the desk says so under its pooled table — hg-v987
- **The standing crypto instruction asks for more indicators and strategies in setup formation. Before adding one, this pack asks which of the reads OMNIROUTE ALREADY scores by carry any out-of-sample information on its own book.** The v701 replay stamped every one of them on all **2,833** walked trades (2026-06-13 → 2026-09-11): the **18 solidity pillars**, the **solidity gates** (families · live-fresh · tape · agreement count), solidity grade GOOD, the legacy tier, the live-price grade at signal, 1h and daily alignment, the same-bar cluster tag, the conviction cert, the 20× stop band and three stop-width bands, and six sessions. `scripts/omniroute-factor-separation.mjs` judges each through the **one** separation method this repo keeps (hg-v984's `separate`, the hg-v920 rule): rows cut into **four windows that share no trade**, the cohort against its complement in each on **win rate AND gross R AND net R**; a **VERDICT** needs all four to agree on all three. The offline refit (`HG_OMNI_REFIT`, not-predictive, test AUC 0.519) had asked whether the pillar **score** ranks winners; this asks each read on its own.
- **Out of sample: ZERO verdicts.** Of **36** measured reads, none is better or worse than its complement in all four windows on win, gross and net together. The only unanimous separator is the module's own baked demotion (**`kindDemoted`**, n=1,634, net **−0.375R** against **+0.001R**, worse 4/4) — and it was **fitted on this window** by the v531 roster bake, so it is flagged **IN-SAMPLE**, offered as no confirmation, and kept beside the rest because it shows the method is not blind on this book.
- **Nine LEANS, named as leans** (net unanimous in all four, win or gross not — the hg-v945 distinction): **momentum convergence ≥ half its max** (n=1,290, net −0.105R vs −0.309R, better w/g/n **2/3/4**), the **live-fresh gate** and the **FRESH live grade** (2/3/4 each), **ASIA** session (3/3/4), the **regime pillar ≥ half** (worse on net 4/4 while *better* on win 3/4), the **same-bar cluster** (worse, 1/0/0), and the three **stop bands** — where a stop at or over 2% reads better on net in **4/4** and **WORSE on win in every window**: cost arithmetic (`costR = rtCost / stopPct`, hg-v919), not setup quality, and the row carries both figures so a reader sees the trade. **A lean is not a verdict, and nothing is wired to one.**
- **What the replay could not measure is said, not folded into "no separation":** four pillars (`fvg`, `atrExpansion`, `orderFlow`, `newsCalendar`) are **constant on every replay row** — the stamps were starved of inputs the live desk has (hg-v533) — and are reported **STARVED**; three (`riskReward`, `liquidationRecovery`, `riskAdjusted`) clear half their max on **every** row and are **DEGENERATE**: no complement to compare against. **One fill bound**, said on the panel: this artifact carries no same-bar ambiguity flag, so the lower bound (hg-v918) cannot be read here.
- **Baked and rendered, not remembered:** `HG_OMNI_FACTOR_SEP` in `omniroute.js` is written by the generator (`--write`; the read-only run is the drift check and exits 1 on drift; a missing marker is fatal, never skipped — the hg-v921 rule), and **`hgOmniFactorSepHtml`** renders it under the pooled table beside the forward panel: the NONE branch today, the in-sample demotion named as no confirmation, the leans as leans, the starved and degenerate reads as unmeasured, and a **VERDICT branch** so a future bake that finds one announces it as a measurement, not a gate. The literal is read by the renderer and by **nothing else** (asserted in code). `npm run omni:factor-sep` is the drift check.
- **So the additive well on this desk is dry on its own evidence, and the two leans worth a forward measurement are named:** momentum convergence and the live-fresh read. Neither is fed to a gate here — an unmeasured veto is the hg-v966 trap, and a lean that fails on win rate is exactly what a shrinking target would produce. **No gate is loosened, no threshold moves, no setup leaves any board.**
- Tests: `tests/test-omniroute-factor-separation.mjs` (45 assertions; **16/16** behavioural mutations caught across `scripts/omniroute-factor-separation.mjs` and `omniroute.js`, baseline verified green before every pass).

### CONTRACT REPORT dated its forward record on the clock bar with the 4h series in hand, and dropped the funding its plan read; a behavioural census drives ten crypto forward-log writers through their real scans and names the one it cannot reach — hg-v986
- **hg-v985 found two dead crypto forward logs by DRIVING the scans rather than reading them** (OI FLOW had never written a record; SQUEEZE's mark never landed). A textual pin proves the shape of a record literal and nothing about whether the code around it reaches that literal, throws before it, or hands it a direction in the wrong case. So this pack drives every crypto writer it can — **OMNIROUTE, OMNIPRESENT, SUPER BEST, SUPER SNIPER, REVERSAL SNIPER, TRENDMX, the GATES tab, DEX SCREENER, PINE and CONTRACT REPORT** — through its real scan with `hg-forward.js` loaded, on a tape whose last closed bar is **one bar behind the clock**, and requires **one record dated on that bar, with a mark, in lower case**, and nothing thrown into `hgFwdWarn`. Nine were sound (OMNIPRESENT records TRIGGERED zones only, so the fixture sweeps the zone; the first probe read its ARMED board as a dead log, which it was not).
- **CONTRACT REPORT was not.** `planFrom` composes its one plan on the **last closed 4h bar** of the series the report is handed, and the record it then wrote carried the plan's mark (hg-v981) and **nothing that said which bar it was** — so `hgFwdRecordScan` dated it on the **floor of the clock**: the hg-v978 defect on a crypto desk. Driven on rows whose last closed bar sits three 4h bars behind the clock: the record read the clock bar. Settlement walks **strictly after** `barT`, so a bar right after the signal that traded through T1 **could not settle** the clock-dated record (reproduced through the ledger rule on a hand-dated record) and **does** settle the bar-dated one; and a FULL REPORT pressed across a clock boundary on the **same** closed bar wrote a **second record** (it writes none now, and the next closed bar is a new record). **The ticker funding `planFrom` read never reached the record either:** hg-v985 named this desk as having "no figure in reach at its record site" — true of that site, and the plan one function up had the ticker all along.
- **The plan says which bar it was composed on and what it read:** `out.barT` is the last row's time (seconds, as the rows carry them; **null** when unreadable or zero — the epoch is not a read), `out.fundingPct` is the ticker's rate as **a number or nothing** (`null`, a string, `NaN` and absent all read NOT RECORDED; exactly zero is a READ zero; a long at +0.05% reads AGAINST through the one shared rule). `hgContractReportRecord` forwards both and the ledger derives `fundAgainst` from `hgFundingAgainstMark`. An unreadable bar falls back to the clock exactly as before. The dedup note on the file says which bar dedups now, and **the hg-v985 census guard moves CONTRACT REPORT from the bare list to the carriers.**
- **EDGE is NOT driven, and the reason is checked rather than remembered:** `edgeScanList` records `found` only; on the seed where `edgeSignal` fires (EMA9 PULLBACK) `edgeAssess` declines the tally, `found` is empty and the block is unreachable on a synthetic tape — the guard proves the signal fires and the assessment declines on that seed, and the block stays pinned textually (hg-v981, hg-v985). **Two stubs in the census, said:** DEX SCREENER's grader returns a ticket (the writer records tickets only and the 35-gate grader does not clear on a synthetic tape), and PINE gets one injected script that fires FRESH (the writer records fresh signals only). The writers under test are the record maps, not the graders.
- **A fixture that pinned the defect, caught by the suite, not by me:** `tests/test-contract-report.mjs` dated its rows in **November 2023** and asserted the recorded plan *sits open* — which held only while the record was dated on the clock; dated on the bar it was composed on, a 2023 bar is **stale**, not open. The rows are dated relative to the clock now, and the file says why.
- **No gate is loosened, no threshold moves, no setup leaves any board.** Records already written under `SEARCH-REPORT` keep their clock-bar dates and settle as they always did.
- Tests: `tests/test-contract-report-ledger-bar.mjs` (52 assertions; **11/11** behavioural mutations caught on `contract-report.js`, baseline verified green before every pass; the census guard catches 6 of the same 11 and is asked about the bar and the mark, not the funding) and `tests/test-crypto-forward-census.mjs` (71 assertions).

### The directional funding rule is applied by the SWING/SCALP matrices, asked by none of the crypto desks that record most, and measured nowhere; every crypto record that had funding in hand carries the rate and the verdict now, the two pure-price desks say so, and two desks whose forward log never worked are repaired — hg-v985
- **`fundingGateDirectional` (hg-setup-core.js) is the SWING/SCALP G4 rule:** funding running against the trade at or beyond **0.04%/interval** vetoes; |funding| over 0.30% is a broken feed. OMNIROUTE's own soft `funding` gate reads the same figure at **0.05** ("crowded on our side") — two bars, one figure, and nothing had ever said whether either separates. **CRYPTOVERSE and 90PERCENT** had the venue's rate on every universe item (`item.fundingPct`, Delta; CoinDCX reports none) and read price alone; **OMNIROUTE, SQUEEZE, OI FLOW, EDGE, CRYPTO SCAN and the GATES tab** had it in hand at the record site and recorded none of it. No artifact records funding beside an outcome (the OMNIROUTE replay fetched a funding leg into `scripts/.bt-cache`, which is not committed), so the question is answerable only forward. Same shape as hg-v984, for the same reason: a new veto unmeasured is the hg-v966 trap and a rule unmeasured forever is the hg-v955 one.
- **One rule, three states, beside the gate it delegates to:** **`hgFundingAgainstMark(fundingPct, dir)`** says whether the rule in force would have VETOED (`true`), let the plan through (`false`) or could not say (`undefined`: no finite rate, no direction, or a feed reading out of range — a broken feed is not a read, and `gateResult`'s degrade would otherwise turn n/a into a veto, right for a gate and wrong for a measurement). It equals the gate's verdict on every readable input (22/22 asserted). **The ledger carries `fundingPct`** (a number, never coerced — `+null` is 0, a real rate — and a rate of exactly zero is a READ zero) **and `fundAgainst`** (the two booleans). `hgFwdRecordScan` stamps the verdict from the one rule whenever a desk hands in a rate; a desk hands in **only the rate**, because the ledger derives the verdict through the same rule the card used and a second hand-in of the verdict would be a duplicated check (the first cut had one on both desks and it survived mutation, as a duplicate must). `hgFwdFold` carries **`fa` / `fw`**; **`hgFwdFundingSplit`** reads the settled records at the rule bar plus the folded counts, and — because the raw rate rides on the record — splits the live records at **OMNIROUTE's 0.05 crowded bar** too, saying that split has no aggregate; the **shared forward panel** prints **FUNDING RULE SPLIT** wherever records carry the mark and nothing where none do.
- **Nine record sites hand the rate in**, each the figure it already had: CRYPTOVERSE and 90PERCENT the universe item's; **SQUEEZE** the tick it classified on; **OI FLOW** the Binance funding it scored; **EDGE** the item its enricher scored (`funding tailwind` / `crowded against trade`); **OMNIROUTE** the very `positioning.fundingPct` its own funding gate read; **CRYPTO SCAN** the item it only ever read as a boolean flag; **the GATES tab** the candidate's, kept beside the state row (the survivor shape is BRAIN's contract); and **`hgCryptoCandRow`** puts the ticker's funding (the Binance twin when the venue reports none, hg-v197) on the candidate row the SWING/SCALP/BEST publish spreads. **The census is derived by call shape** and names the ten writers with **no figure in reach** at their record site — OMNIPRESENT, DEX SCREENER, REVERSAL SNIPER, BRAIN, TRENDMX, both PINE lanes, CONTRACT REPORT, SUPER BEST, SUPER SNIPER — rather than leaving them to memory. **CRYPTOVERSE and 90PERCENT print `FUNDING AGAINST +0.0700%`** with a note naming the matrices that veto it and saying this desk does not, and **gate on nothing**: through both real tabs, the same tapes at 0.07%, 0.01% and 0.00% form the **identical board**; Delta longs read AGAINST, Delta shorts read with, CoinDCX rows read NOT RECORDED.
- **Found on the way, three defects in shipped forward logs. (1) SQUEEZE's hg-v981 mark never landed:** the published row carries no candles (the snapshot is persisted), so `hgFwdLastBar(fr.rows4h)` read `undefined` on every record since hg-v981 — the census saw the carrier text and never drove the scan. The source result now rides **beside** the published row at the same index (the engine.js `svBar` shape), and a **real fired scan** proves mark, bar and funding on the record. **(2) OI FLOW has never written a forward record**, and its FORWARD panel has never painted: the record block and the panel referenced **`W`** in a file whose alias is **`G`** — from the day the block was written (686a2cc) — so the `try` threw on its first line and the `catch` swallowed it into `hgFwdWarn`. **(3)** Behind that, OI FLOW's classifier emits **`LONG` / `SHORT`** and `hgFwdNormalize` accepts only lower case, so the fixed block would still have written nothing; the desk lower-cases the same field at every other read. All three are repaired, proved through a **real OI FLOW scan** (two records, both directions, the mark riding), and the panel now **repaints after every publish** instead of at mount only — a scan that had just recorded used to leave *Nothing recorded yet* on screen until the next mount. A repo-wide sweep finds no other ledger writer referencing an alias it does not define.
- **A fixture correction to my own hg-v984 guard, said here:** its 90PERCENT section fired setups only on the CoinDCX rows (the Delta rows never fire through the tab path under Delta costs); its assertions held on those rows and were weaker than they read. This pack's fixtures are seeded through the tab path under each venue's cost.
- **No gate is loosened, no threshold moves, no setup leaves any board.** Records already written carry no rate and settle as they always did.
- Tests: `tests/test-crypto-funding-mark.mjs` (102 assertions; **44/44** behavioural mutations caught across `hg-setup-core.js`, `hg-forward.js`, `cryptoverse.js`, `ninetypercent.js`, `squeeze.js`, `oiflow.js`, `edge.js`, `omniroute.js`, `cryptoscan.js`, `engine.js` and `index.html`, baseline verified green before every pass).

### The crypto macro alt filter is applied on six desks, asked by none of the crypto desks that record most, and measured nowhere; the ledger records its verdict on every crypto record now, and the two pure-price desks say so — hg-v984
- **`hgMacroAllowsCrypto` (plans.js) blocks an alt long while BTC.D > 55% or the dollar is rising.** SWING, SCALP, EDGE, BEST, SETUP CONFIRM and SUPER SETUP apply it. **OMNIROUTE, OMNIPRESENT, SQUEEZE, OI FLOW, DEX SCREENER, CRYPTO SCAN, CRYPTOVERSE and 90PERCENT do not** — and **no artifact in the repo records the filter's verdict beside an outcome**, so nobody can say whether it pays. Under the crypto instruction the two obvious moves are both wrong: wiring it onto those desks is a new veto with no measurement of what it removes (the hg-v966 trap), and leaving it unmeasured forever is the hg-v955 one. CRYPTOVERSE and 90PERCENT are the sharpest case — Delta + CoinDCX perp desks that read **price alone** (no funding, no OI, no liquidation, no CVD, no macro) and write `ticket: true` records into the ledger, so an alt long they form while BTC.D reads 58% is a plan every other crypto desk would refuse, recorded as tradeable.
- **One rule, three states, one home (hg-v949):** **`hgMacroAltMark(sym, dir)`** in `plans.js` says whether the filter **in force** would have BLOCKED a plan (`true`), let it through (`false`) or could not say (`undefined`: no direction, a gold-lane symbol — a crypto dominance rule has nothing to say about XAUUSD — or a regime snapshot with **neither** gauge readable). The gate itself fails open on that snapshot, which is right for a gate and wrong for a measurement; a cold-start record must read NOT RECORDED, never "allowed". The mark equals NOT the gate's verdict on every readable input (20/20 asserted), so it is never a second rule. **The forward ledger stamps it on every crypto record at `hgFwdRecordScan`** from that one rule — **26 record maps needed no edit** — a desk that read it hands it in and wins (a non-boolean hand-in is ignored, and the shared read decides); `hgFwdNormalize` keeps only the two booleans (`1`, `0`, `'no'`, `''`, `{}` are NOT RECORDED); `hgFwdFold` carries **`mb` / `ma`** buckets so the split outlives the live cap; **`hgFwdMacroSplit`** / **`hgFwdMacroSplitHtml`** read it, and the **shared forward panel** prints **MACRO ALT FILTER SPLIT · N of M marked settled records fired where the filter would have BLOCKED · blocked xR at h% · allowed yR at h% · U carry no mark and are counted as NEITHER** on every desk whose records carry the mark, and **nothing** where none does (every gold tab; every crypto record written before this).
- **CRYPTOVERSE and 90PERCENT read it on the card, and gate on nothing:** each takes the read once per setup, prints **MACRO FILTER WOULD BLOCK** with the reason (`BTC.D 58.1% > 55% — risk-off for alt longs`) and a note naming the desks that refuse the plan and saying this one does not, and hands **the same read** to its record so card and record agree by construction. Proved through both **real tabs** on the same tapes: under a risk-off read every long carries the chip and its record reads BLOCKED, every short reads allowed; under a benign read **the same number of setups form with identical entry, stop and target** and no chip; with no snapshot the board is again identical and every record is NOT RECORDED. Neither desk calls the gate, a regime veto or the post-gate veto (asserted).
- **Found on the way, two defects:** **(1)** `hgBtcdPct` read a **null** BTC.D gauge as **0%** — `+null` is 0 — harmless to the gate (0 never blocks) and wrong for anything that asks whether the leg was READ; null means unread now, and the gate's fail-open on a dark snapshot is asserted unchanged. **(2)** `hgTicketFinalGates` carried the gold-lane regex inline; it reads the one predicate **`hgIsGoldLaneSym`** now, and the regex appears exactly once in the file.
- **And the nearest measured crypto-macro gate IS measured, which is why no veto is fed:** OMNIROUTE's `regime` row (RISK-ON / MIXED / RISK-OFF, soft since hg-v698) had its verdict recorded on **all 2,833** walked trades of the v701 replay. On **four disjoint windows** (`scripts/omniroute-regime-separation.mjs`, reusing hg-v920's `splitDisjoint`): **adverse-veto (n=691) carries NO verdict** — worse in **2 of 4** on win, gross and net, whole book **−0.232R inside against −0.211R outside**; any adverse read or the label against the trade **3 of 4**; symbol-trend misalignment **1/2/3**. The module's own baked kind demotion separates **4 of 4** (−0.375R against +0.001R), which shows the method is not blind on this book — and it is flagged **IN-SAMPLE**, fitted on an overlapping window by the v531 roster bake, so it is offered as no confirmation. A crypto macro read that separates nothing on the one desk where it can be checked is not fed to nine more desks as a veto; the live mark is how the alt filter itself gets the same test.
- **No gate is loosened, no threshold moves, no setup leaves any board.** Records already written carry no mark and settle as they always did.
- Tests: `tests/test-crypto-macro-mark.mjs` (109 assertions; **41/42** behavioural mutations caught across `plans.js`, `hg-forward.js`, `cryptoverse.js`, `ninetypercent.js` and `scripts/omniroute-regime-separation.mjs`, the 42nd — the mark's guard on the gate's own `unchecked` shape, unreachable through the mark's earlier guards — proved **equivalent on 468 inputs**, baseline verified green before every pass).

### The solidity grader's measured-edge gates judged on the actual-tally expectancy, and no fill-aware expectancy existed for them to read — hg-v983
- **`hgSolGateMeasuredEdge` (G6, veto under −0.25R) and `hgSolGateMeasuredWinning` (G7, promotion at or over +0.5R) grade the cards on OMNIROUTE, REVERSAL SNIPER, DEX SCREENER, OMNIGOLD, NEW GOLD and GOLD PINE** from `hgFwdStats`' `expR` — the expectancy of the **actual** tally, which settles a resting order the tape never reached as a win or a loss (34.3% of what OMNIROUTE opens never fills, hg-v981). hg-v981 gave those records a mark and hg-v982 gave the two mechanic judges one fill-aware rule — and the ledger carried a fill-aware **hit** and no fill-aware **expectancy**, so the one gate that judges expectancy had nothing to read. hg-v982 said so and refused to wire a reader with nothing to read; this pack gives it the figure first.
- **`hgFwdStats` carries `fillExpR` and `fillAvgRr`**, mirroring `expR` term for term (NaN with no fill-settled record, exactly −1 when none won, else `hit × avgRr − (1 − hit)`), summed through the **aggregate fold** too (`fillRrSum` on both the gate-clear and shown buckets) so records the live list prunes keep deciding; an aggregate written before this pack reads NaN, never a fabricated zero. **`hgFwdJudgeSample` carries the expectancy of whichever sample it chose** (the actual one, or the fill-aware one when that clears the floor on its own), and **G6 and G7 read it through one local seam** (`hgSolJudge`), labelling the reason line **`measured −0.40R over 25 FILLED samples · 12 never filled, excluded`**; a fill-aware sample with no readable expectancy is not preferred, and with `hg-forward.js` absent the grader reads the actual tally exactly as it always did. The KIND PERFORMANCE fill line prints the fill-aware expectancy beside the hit. **Neither the grader nor the two desk judges compute a fill expectancy of their own** (asserted).
- **Driven end to end:** 12 OMNIROUTE-shaped resting records — 4 filled and won, 4 filled and lost, 4 never filled and **settled as wins by the actual tally** — read **+1.667R actual against +1.000R on the orders that opened**; the grader on 47 settled at +0.30R **passes**, and the same 47 of which 25 opened at −0.40R **VETOES**; 60 settled at +0.72R **promote** on the actual tally and the 40 that opened at +0.30R **do not**; 19 fill-aware records under the floor leave the actual tally deciding, unlabelled.
- **No threshold moves.** The only verdict that can change is one a fill-aware sample at or over the grader's own floor (20) overturns, and no crypto record written before hg-v981 carries one — so no board moves today.
- Tests: `tests/test-crypto-fill-expectancy.mjs` (38 assertions; **16/16** behavioural mutations caught across `hg-forward.js`, `hg-solidity.js` and `hg-perf-panel.js`, baseline verified green before every pass).

### OMNIROUTE's out-of-sample judge counted every resting order as if it had opened; the fill-aware rule OMNIGOLD carried inline is one rule now, and both desks read it — hg-v982
- **OMNIROUTE's measured-edge gate vetoes a mechanic when the tickets this ledger cleared read at or under −2σ against breakeven out of sample.** Every order this desk records **rests** at the setup level (hg-v424); its committed replay never fills on the signal bar and leaves **34.3%** of what it opens unfilled (hg-v981) — and the judge read the **actual** tally, which settles a resting order the tape never reached as a target hit or a stop. Since hg-v981 the desk's records carry a mark, so the fill-aware tally exists in `hgFwdStats`; **nothing on OMNIROUTE read it.** OMNIGOLD's judge has preferred it since the fill model shipped, in an inline block of its own — two desks, one ledger, two rules.
- **One rule, one home (hg-v949):** **`hgFwdJudgeSample(stats, minN)`** in `hg-forward.js` reads the chosen population's actual tally and prefers its **fill-aware** tally only when that clears the **same floor on its own**, so a log that is mostly legacy records decides exactly as before rather than on a handful of new ones; a fill count with no readable hit rate is not a sample (the `+null` trap). OMNIGOLD's inline block is replaced by the call and its labels are untouched; OMNIROUTE's ticket-only judge reads it and labels **`N FILLED TICKETS · hit% (U never filled, excluded)`**. **Neither desk reads `fillSamples` itself any more**, and the guard asserts so.
- **Driven on both real gates** (`hgOmniGates`, `hgOgGates`, each handed a forward record): 30 cleared tickets at 50% pass; the **same 30, of which only 22 ever opened and those 22 lost, VETO** — the 8 that never filled were settled as wins the tape never gave anybody — naming *22 FILLED TICKETS · 9% T1-first (8 never filled, excluded)*; 19 fill-aware records under the floor leave the actual tally deciding, exactly as before; a fill-aware tally that pays passes, labelled FILLED.
