The first two were forming **nothing** and now paint as demoted cards; the last two can now lead. **None is unanimous on four disjoint windows** (1/4, 1/4, 3/4, 3/4) and `bosalign` flips sign at all three walk-forward splits — each row says so. **Two further flips the rule would make are REFUSED** (`p5vwap` n=6, `vpbook` n=1): both **tighten**, so they'd show fewer setups on the thinnest evidence, and **the demote bar has no sample floor** — a real gap in the rule — while `vpbook`'s own `why` already says it is "far too thin to demote on". **Reversible:** each moved row keeps `actionBaked`; `hgGoldEdgeAction` resolves the verdict in force and `hgGoldSetupEdgeApply` branches on that, so `hgGoldSetEdgeRetune(false)` truly restores the originals. Tests: `tests/test-gold-edge-retune.mjs`.
- **GOLD SCALP was populated all along — the banner above the cards was the problem (hg-v940):** Driven end to end (mount → click `#gsRun` → read the **final** DOM, not the accumulated writes) the tab was already carrying **two cards with full levels** — XM XAUUSD SHORT grade B, `EMA RIBBON PULLBACK` and `S52 RANGE-BAR S0 SWEEP` — 31,539 chars of rendered cards, `#gsEmpty` `display:none`. What said otherwise was the combined banner directly above them: *"no tape-aligned engine plan on SCALP — stand aside"*. **Why:** `hgGoldUniformCompose` rejects through **seven silent `continue`s**, and both setups died at the third (**demoted**) because the hg-v930 **one-at-a-time** hold had withheld them while two gold convictions were live — the stat line said so, the banner did not. It emitted one of two fixed strings depending only on whether a tape had been read, so **a desk that composed two setups and withheld them read exactly like a desk that found none**. **The fix is disclosure only:** every rule now counts, a candidate is counted **once at the first rule that rejected it** so the buckets **partition** the input (the guard asserts they sum to it), and the message names the largest bucket with the true total — *"2 engine candidates on SCALP, all demoted (held back, cannot lead) — stand aside"*. Ties break from *the desk withheld it* toward *the plan was unusable*. **The tape is named only when the tape did the work** — blaming it for a demote was the old message's actual error. **No gate, threshold or verdict moves:** the guard asserts a clean tape-aligned grade-A candidate still leads and an against-tape candidate is still HELD and shown (hg-v593). The banner is shared, so **GOLD SWING and OMNIGOLD** get the same honesty. Direct mint for reference: 108 candidates over 120 scans, 0 throws, 15 mechanics; 294 of 385 rejects are *structure too close* — hg-v929's finding that the R:R floor is not what empties this board, and it is not touched. Tests: `tests/test-gold-stand-aside-why.mjs`.
- **MILLI GOLD showed nothing — two defects hg-v938 shipped, found by driving the tab end to end (hg-v939):** **(1) never-scanned and scanned-and-empty were the same state.** v938 read `__og.lastView` and returned null without it — but OMNIGOLD sets `lastView = null` **deliberately** on its honest-empty branch (the hg-v540 comment says so), meaning *scanned, nothing to show*. Conflating them told the reader to wait for something that had already happened **and asked OMNIGOLD to refresh on every paint** — a scan storm on exactly the quiet tape where an empty board is normal. `__og.ran` is now the authority; `lastView` only supplies cards, so an empty board returns an **empty list with `ran:true`** and only a never-run desk returns null. The tab says "OMNIGOLD scanned and its board is empty — the desk being quiet, not this tab waiting". **(2) The fallback could not start a scan.** v938 called `tab.refresh()`, and `refreshOmnigold` returns `'skipped: not run yet'` on a desk that has never run — a no-op in exactly the case it existed for, so opening MILLI GOLD without OMNIGOLD waited forever. It now calls **`hgScanOneTab('omnigold')`**, the shell's own entry, which runs `HG_TAB_AUTO_SCAN` and clicks the real RUN button when a module refresh skips. **Proved end to end:** with the RUN path driven, `ran` true, 3 cards exported, tab reported 3 scanned / 0 on the roster. **And the v938 fix is now measured:** the same bars through the old starved path give **16.5 UNCHECKED gates per card (28%)**; through OMNIGOLD's fed cards, **8.0 per card (14%)** — the rest are feeds genuinely absent offline, which is what UNCHECKED should mean. One assertion of mine survived mutation (it grepped for `hgScanOneTab`, which the explanatory comment also contains) and is now **behavioural** — the tab is run against a stub shell and the call observed. No threshold moves, no gate loosens, roster and forward result unchanged. Tests: `tests/test-milli-gold.mjs`.
- **MILLI GOLD was LESS gated than the desk it claimed to be a subset of (hg-v938):** hg-v936 stated — in the tab's panel and its file header — that nothing was loosened because the roster only ever *removes*. True of the roster, **false in practice**: the tab called `hgOgDetect` / `hgOgEvaluate` **itself with an empty context**. OMNIGOLD hands its evaluator ~20 fields (daily/4h bars, macro, DXY, yields, ADR, news, live + market price, zone context, PAXG basis, quote, L2, bid, ask, scan clock, pooled stats); without them **~14 gates per card fail open to UNCHECKED** — `level-fresh · fill-path · htf-daily · macro-realrate · dxy-inverse · yield-guard · adr-budget · news-window · fill-risk · zone-anchor · weekend-exposure · spot-basis` — every scan, by construction. **Found by the desk owner reading a card.** **The fix is structural:** rebuilding that context here is the second copy that drifts, so the tab now reads the cards OMNIGOLD has **already evaluated** (new export **`hgOgLastCards`**, by reference so gate ledgers survive) and keeps the roster ones. No second context because **no second scan** — "every gate is OMNIGOLD's, fully fed" is now true by construction. With no scan available it **asks OMNIGOLD to refresh and waits**, never a thinner scan of its own; the guard asserts it does not reach for the detector even as a fallback *name*, since a `gfn()` lookup is a call a paren-check misses. **The claim is now measured:** the tab counts its own ledger and prints PASS / AGAINST / VETO / **UNCHECKED** with the unchecked share — that count would have made the original defect visible on day one. `hgOgLastRows` is exported too, so the tape rule judges the **series on screen**, timeframe derived from the bars rather than assumed. One stale exemption came off `test-gold-family-scan` (the tab now moves with the feed). Nothing about the roster, gates, thresholds or the forward result changes — this pack makes the v936 claim **true** rather than restating it. Tests: `tests/test-milli-gold.mjs`.
- **MILLI GOLD backtested and forward-tested — the roster beats the desk unanimously and still loses (hg-v937):** hg-v936 disclosed that its roster was chosen on the whole walk, then **led the tab with that roster's in-sample figure anyway**, calling it an upper bound. This pack measured the bound: it is **negative**. **Backtest:** all at once **+0.0643R / +0.0527R** at the two fill bounds vs a desk at **−0.1160R / −0.2864R**; **one position at a time +0.0153R** at both bounds vs **−0.0226R / −0.3744R**. All in-sample. **Forward test** — roster rebuilt on the *earlier* part of the walk and judged on what came after, three anchored splits × both bounds: it beats the full OMNIGOLD book in **6 of 6 trials, unanimously**, and is net-positive in **0 of 6**, running **−0.1673R to −0.0390R**. **So selecting the mechanics that have paid is measurably better than not selecting, and still loses on every slice that came after the choice.** "Beats the desk" and "pays" are tracked as separate claims throughout. **The selection bias is now sized:** only **6–7 of the 9** shipped mechanics are re-chosen without sight of the whole walk. The shipped roster scores +0.0939 / +0.0373 / +0.0171 on those tails and is **contaminated** there by construction — printed beside the honest number, marked, and the guard asserts the verdict expression never reads it. **The tab now leads with the forward result**, names the in-sample figure as the one to distrust, and with none baked **refuses to quote it on its own**. `HG_MILLI_FORWARD` is generated by `scripts/milli-gold-walk.mjs`; `npm run gold:milli-walk` is the drift check. The mutation pass forced one design fix: the verdict was inline and, because all six trials agree, `every`→`some` survived — a rule that would accept **one** agreeing split. It is now an exported function exercised on mixed trials. **No threshold moves, no gate loosens, and the roster is unchanged** — this pack measured what shipped and corrected what the tab claims about it. Tests: `tests/test-milli-gold-walk.mjs`.
- **MILLI GOLD — a tab that forms only from the mechanics that measured profitable (hg-v936):** **`milligold.js`**, in the GOLD nav group. The roster is **derived, not chosen**: a mechanic is admitted when its **gate-clear** record is net-positive at XM on ≥ 20 firings. **Nine of 54 qualify** — `P5-DRIVE · STRUCT-BOS · SQUEEZE-FIRE · CUSUM-SHIFT · MMOVE · TREND-RECLAIM · P6-COMP · EQH-SWEEP · BOS-RETEST` — pooling to **678 settled trades, gross +0.0887R, net +0.0565R at XM**. The sample floor **is OMNIGOLD's own `MIN_SAMPLES`** and the guard asserts the two are the same number. **It is not a new engine:** every detector, gate, plan and grade is OMNIGOLD's, via `hgOgFetchRows` / `hgOgDetect` / `hgOgEvaluate`; the only change is **which** mechanics may reach a card, and the filter runs on the **detect** output so an off-roster mechanic is never scored, graded or ranked. **Nothing is loosened** — the roster only ever *removes*, so G1–G7, the cost ceiling, the one-at-a-time hold and the measured-edge veto all still apply. **The disclosure leads the tab**, because "the profitable ones" is exactly the shape a reader over-trusts: hg-v935 tested this **exact** selection out of sample and it scored **13–14/16, never unanimous**, so +0.0565R is what these nine did *in the book they were chosen from* — an **upper bound, not a forecast** — and each is inside the noise alone (largest |t| **1.55** against a family bar above 3.1). **An empty roster shows nothing** and never falls back to the full register. Every card carries its mechanic's record and calls it **in sample**; a quiet scan says silence is **by construction**. Generated by `scripts/milli-gold-roster.mjs`, which **refuses to write an empty roster**; `npm run gold:milli` is the drift check. Tests: `tests/test-milli-gold.mjs`.
- **Selecting mechanics by their own record — measured, and REFUSED (hg-v935):** OMNIGOLD forms setups from **every** registered mechanic and its measured-edge gate only vetoes at **−2σ**, which almost nothing reaches — so a mechanic whose own record is negative still tickets. GOLD SCALP got a suppress/demote table in hg-v928; OMNIGOLD never did. **The obvious test is worthless:** ranking 54 mechanics on the whole book and keeping the winners, then scoring on that same book, cannot fail — hg-v920 caught that shape once already. So **the selection never sees the window it is judged on**: four disjoint windows, mechanics ranked on the **other three**, the chosen set scored on the held-out one — four out-of-sample trials, both fill bounds, **16 agreements needed**. **The obvious rule fails:** "keep the gross-positive mechanics" scores **13–14/16** at every sample floor, never unanimous, with or without dropping the too-thin. The premise that this desk can pick its winners is **not supported**. **The weak rule is unanimous and is still refused:** dropping only mechanics at or below a bar is unanimous at some bars — and switches **on and off between neighbours** (−0.05 ✓ · −0.10 ✓ · **−0.15 ✗ · −0.20 ✗** · −0.25 ✓ · −0.30 ✓ · −0.40 ✗), **4 unanimous cells in 2 separate runs**. A real effect fades as the bar moves; one that flickers is a search over bars — hg-v922's stop-width finding restated. **Unanimity alone does not ship; it must be CONTIGUOUS**, and that requirement is in the code (`contiguous()` gates `out.ships`), not the commentary. **The ceiling answers the question that prompted it:** the best unanimous cell lifts net by **+0.0299R** and leaves the book at **−0.0861R** on 8,132 filled plans — mechanic selection, read as generously as the evidence allows, does not make this desk pay, it makes it less negative. **Nothing is gated on any of it** — no mechanic dropped, no threshold moved, the Šidák bar still corrects over the whole register. `HG_OG_SELECTION` is **generated** by `scripts/mechanic-selection.mjs` (a refusal quoted from memory drifts exactly as a promotion does), and `hgOgSelectionRefusedHtml` **branches on the verdict** so a future bake that finds a contiguous rule is announced rather than hidden behind today's answer. Re-derive: `npm run og:selection`. Tests: `tests/test-mechanic-selection.mjs` — which proves leave-one-window-out **behaviourally**, with a mechanic that wins only inside one window and must not be selected for it, plus the mirror showing it *would* be kept if trained on that window.
- **A new detector has no record — say whose record it is, and refuse the one that is measured failing (hg-v934):** hg-v933 stamped all three of its detectors **NO MEASURED RECORD**. True of the idea and **false about the evidence**: `goldround`'s OMNIGOLD twin **ROUND-MAGNET has 587 settled firings** in the committed walk and `goldfix`'s twin **LONDON-FIX has 69**. Telling a reader nothing is known, when hundreds of measurements of the nearest thing exist, is the same failure as quoting them as if they were this desk's. Each detector now names its twin and quotes that twin's **gate-clear** record (hg-v917's honest line, worse than the unscoped one for 42 of 54 mechanics), **attributed every time** to OMNIGOLD's gates and its 1h horizon. **Two more ports:** **`goldwopen`** (weekly open sweep + reclaim — gold reopens Sunday 22:00 UTC and OMNIGOLD has read WEEKLY-OPEN since round five; sweep and reclaim only, since price merely trading above the weekly open is not a setup, it is Tuesday) and **`goldfib`** (61.8 retrace hold — the impulse is measured on bars **strictly before** the trigger bar, so the bar being judged cannot define the level it is judged against). **THE THIRD PORT IS REFUSED and it is the point of the pack:** **PIVOT-REJECT** is the last mechanic these tabs lack and it is not unmeasured — 159 settled firings, **23.3%** to T1 first against a **33.3%** breakeven at 2R, gross **−0.3026R**, net **−0.3727R** at XM, **−2.69σ** naive / **−2.63σ** cluster-robust, past `EDGE_VETO_Z` which hg-v925 deliberately left untouched. And **GOLD SCALP / GOLD SWING have no measured-edge gate** — OMNIGOLD refuses to ticket it and these desks have nothing that would, so porting it would move a vetoed mechanic onto the one place in the gold stack that cannot veto it. The guard is **behavioural**, not the accident that nothing mints it. **OMNIGOLD:** `hgOgUnobservedPanelHtml` publishes that **24 of 78** registered mechanics have **never fired**, that since hg-v925 they can ticket on no evidence at all, and what they cost the 54 that do have a record — the Šidák bar sits at **3.2128σ** across 78 rather than **3.1056σ** across the testable 54, **+0.1072σ stricter for every measured mechanic**. Narrowing the family to the observed subset is the defensible statistic — a hypothesis never evaluated is not a test — but it **loosens** a bar, so it is **published and not acted on**; `hgOgFamilyZ` still corrects over all 78. **Generated, not transcribed:** `scripts/gold-sibling-records.mjs` writes the record literal from the artifact (the hg-v921 rule), a named twin with no gate-clear record is **fatal, never skipped**, `npm run gold:siblings` is the read-only drift check and the writer is folded into `npm run gold:rebake`. Measured on the real mint, 120 scans: **100 → 108 candidates**, **0 throws**, lead count **unchanged at 10**; they still mint **demoted**, because a quoted twin record is not this desk's record. Tests: `tests/test-gold-sibling-records.mjs`.
- **Three gold-native strategies on GOLD SCALP / GOLD SWING (hg-v933):** **`gold-extra-strategies.js`** — **`goldfix`** (LBMA London fix fade: the 10:30 / 15:00 **London-local**, DST-aware benchmark auctions; fades a run-in that drifted ≥0.8×ATR into the auction and closed back through its midpoint — a continuation mints nothing), **`golddxy`** (dollar divergence: gold and DXY moving **together** with magnitude is the anomaly and reads *with* the metal — the desk held the dollar leg only as a one-way veto and never read the anomaly), **`goldround`** (round-dollar rejection at $100/$50/$25/$10 — OMNIGOLD has had ROUND-MAGNET since it was built and the two tabs a trader works from never did; the **wick-rejection half only**, since the magnet half is a target, not a trigger). **Bars only** — no new feed, and the dollar leg returns null rather than inventing a correlation. **They mint DEMOTED:** a new mechanic has no record and this desk does not let an unmeasured setup lead — the same rule `hgOgSiblingRecordNote` applies to never-observed mechanics. `hgGoldExtraSetPromotable(true)` lifts it, deliberately one call and not a default. **They are NOT registered as OMNIGOLD mechanics,** and that is not an oversight: hg-v923 showed registering one **widens the Šidák bar for every existing mechanic** (77→78 moved it 3.2091→3.2128σ), so three more would tighten every other mechanic's promotion bar on a desk where nothing clears it. **The prior is against more detectors** — hg-v922 found nothing either desk ranks by separates outcomes — which is why the discipline above is the pack, not the count. Measured effect on the real mint: **55 → 100 candidates** over 120 scans (+45 from `goldround`), **0 throws**, and the lead count **unchanged at 10**. Tests: `tests/test-gold-extra-strategies.mjs`.
- **The excursions that were measured and thrown away, and the bars that decide OMNIGOLD's sign (hg-v932):** `scripts/backtest-omnigold.mjs` has computed `tr.mfe` / `tr.mae` on every filled bar since the 2R work — under a comment saying *"no artifact recorded that. mfe/mae are that record"* — and **`settleRecord` never emitted them**. The work was done and dropped on the way out. `backtest-goldscalp.mjs` tracked none at all. So on gold every exit question (is the fixed target right? would a breakeven move pay? is the stop wider than it needs to be?) was not *unresolved* but **unmeasurable**, while crypto has had `hgHeatProfile` / `hgStopSweep` since hg-v182 plus a whole exit-optimisation artifact — which, worth knowing before anyone spends effort there, **found nothing**: five families, best z=2.10 against a Šidák bar of 2.66. **`lib/gold-excursions.mjs`** now holds ONE rule both walks fold through and both emit as `maeR` / `mfeR`. The rule is deliberately conservative — **a bar that touches the stop contributes nothing to the favourable excursion**, because intrabar order is unknown and the walk resolves both-touch stop-first, so crediting the high of the bar that stopped the trade out would let the sweep invent gains no fill saw. Shared rather than copied because the copy that drifts is the one that flatters its own desk. — **`lib/ambiguous-bar.mjs` + `scripts/resolve-ambiguous.mjs`** attack the 672 `both-touch` rows (8.3% of OMNIGOLD's filled book) that hg-v931 showed are the entire reason its win rate reads 30.4% at one fill bound and 38.6% at the other. A finer bar inside the ambiguous one answers which came first; bars are **sorted, not trusted** (an out-of-order feed would otherwise pick the winner), and **a finer bar that itself holds both levels resolves NOTHING** rather than guessing one level down. It runs as its own pass over a committed artifact, so it is re-runnable without a full re-bake, cannot destabilise the walk, and is testable with an injected fetch. **It fails conservative everywhere** — no bars, wrong window, still-ambiguous, a throwing fetch, no exit stamp: the row keeps exactly what the stop-first walk recorded, flagged with the real reason, and the pass **refuses to rewrite an artifact when nothing was resolved**. Run here: all 672 found, 672 unresolved, `HTTP 403`, nothing written — the designed failure mode. Honours `HG_KLINES_BASE` / `HG_KLINES_PROXY` (hg-v921). **No threshold moves and no outcome changes until a re-bake can fetch.** Tests: `tests/test-gold-excursions.mjs`, `tests/test-ambiguous-bar.mjs`.
- **Each gold desk against its own breakeven, and stop-width-as-sizing REFUSED (hg-v931):** A win rate alone is not evidence — shrink the target and it rises for free — so each desk is now reported against the breakeven **its own** planned R:R demands, and the two desks do not plan alike. **GOLD SCALP** 43.4% vs **40.0%** needed (R:R 1.50) = **+3.4 to +4.4 pts, CLEARS at both fill bounds**. **OMNIGOLD** 30.4% vs **33.3%** needed (R:R 2.00) = **−3.0 to +5.3 pts — the SIGN FLIPS**, which is a different claim from "slightly worse": its edge is *unproven* in a nameable way. **The cause is resolution, not strategy:** 672 `both-touch` rows (**8.3%** of OMNIGOLD's filled book) against **23 (0.9%)** on GOLD SCALP, because OMNIGOLD's scalp lane runs on **1h** bars and GOLD SCALP's on **15m**, and a longer bar is likelier to hold stop and target together. Also published: **17.8%** of OMNIGOLD's plans never fill (6.1% on GOLD SCALP). One literal `HG_GOLD_DESK_BREAKEVEN` in `goldind.js` serves both desks (a second copy would drift on re-bake); `hgGoldBreakevenHtml(desk)` renders it, leading the OMNIGOLD panel chain, fail-open when goldind is absent. **Nothing is gated on it.** Re-derive: `node scripts/desk-breakeven.mjs`. — **`scripts/stop-width-sizing.mjs` REFUSES the sizing idea:** hg-v922 found stop width the only unanimous separator and every test since treated it as a *reject*; the untested alternative was to keep the trade and **size** it. Seven schemes × 4 disjoint windows × 2 fill bounds × gross+net (16 tests each): best on GOLD SCALP is `cliff050` at **14/16 while keeping 9% of the risk**; every **graded** scheme (ramp / half-size / sqrt) scores **7–10/16** and none beats the cliffs — so the hypothesis that sizing beats rejecting is **refuted**. `ramp050` improves the pooled whole-book net (−0.157R → −0.091R) and still fails, which is exactly the hg-v920 trap. Expectancy is measured per unit of **risk deployed** so a scheme cannot look good by trading less. **No sizing rule ships.** Tests: `tests/test-desk-breakeven.mjs`, `tests/test-stop-width-sizing.mjs`.
- **One position at a time, on GOLD SCALP too (hg-v930):** hg-v926 measured the only read in the gold evidence base positive at **both fill bounds** — the SCALP ticket book taken one at a time is **+0.1484R** (n=73, conservative) and **+0.4155R** (n=54, the other) against **−0.1160R** all at once (n=8132, XM). The walk publishes **59.4 plans a day** on one instrument and holds **57 at once** — one bet on gold at 57× size. **The fix went in on OMNIGOLD and stopped there:** `hgOgOneAtATimeGate` reads *both* booking stores, so OMNIGOLD held itself back while GOLD SCALP was in a trade and **GOLD SCALP went on stacking**, for four packs. A held card is **DEMOTED** — it paints, keeps its levels and tally, and can never lead; every existing leader picker skips demoted rows, so MOST PROBABLE empties with no second code path. **The handoff is the actual enforcement:** a demote alone does not remove ADD TO BOOK or SEND TO TRADE PLAN (hg-v611 needed a suppress verdict for exactly that, after a demote stamp left four stop-outs bookable), so `cardHTML` drops both CTAs and says why in their place. **The position you are already in keeps running** — a `c.locked` row is skipped and can still lead, which is why the hold runs **after** `applyConviction` and **before** the pick. **Fails open:** `gsOpenGoldConvictions` *delegates* to `hgOgOpenGoldConvictions` so one definition serves both desks; an absent or throwing reader is 0 open and every setup stands. **The limit is on the panel:** n=73 at **+1.16σ** is positive and **not significant**, and SWING disagrees between bounds (−0.240 / +0.317) so it carries no verdict — what is *not* statistical is the concentration, and that is the half this acts on. `gsSetOneAtATime(false)` reverses it; `window.HG_GS_ONE_AT_A_TIME` overrides; resolved per scan so headless warms honour it. **No threshold moved.** Tests: `tests/test-gold-one-at-a-time.mjs`.
- **How far under the floor, not merely which gate (hg-v929):** GOLD SCALP has named its binding gate since hg-v905, and on a quiet tape it is almost always **structure too close — R:R insufficient**. A reader who got that far had exactly one obvious move available — **lower the floor** — and nothing on the page to argue with. Over 120 consecutive scans through the real `goldScalpSetups`, **294 of 385 rejections were this gate and 94.9% of them capped TP1 below 0.80R** against a **1.20R** floor; only **2** sat in [1.10,1.20), so dropping the floor to 1.00R gives back **six of 294**, and to 0.80R, fifteen. **The floor is not what empties the board; opposing structure is.** That measurement is a scratch harness on synthetic bars, so **no number from it is baked** — `gsRrShortfall` reads the numeric **`rr` / `rrFloor`** the reject now carries (`goldind.js`, `goldswing.js` — the swing side from its own floor variable, not a retyped literal) and computes from the scan in front of the reader. **A prose parse was the obvious shortcut and is refused:** it would bind the readout to the wording of a sentence and go blank when that changed — and it is **lossy**, which was proved the hard way: binning the same 294 rows by parsing their sentences gave 92.8% and *eight*, because the sentence prints `toFixed(1)` and an 0.96R cap reads as 1.0R. **Two things it must not overstate, both rendered:** a row dropped here **returned before the later gates ran**, so every count is the *most* a lower floor could give back, never what it would; and a lower floor buys **the same stop for a smaller first target**, not merely more trades. **Two floors are never averaged** — the larger group leads, any other is named separately, ties break on the floor. **This counts; it does not gate:** the 1.2R scalp floor, the swing build floor and G1–G7 are unchanged. Tests: `tests/test-gold-rr-shortfall.mjs`.
- **The age of the evidence that forms every gold setup (hg-v927):** Every suppress/demote/prefer on GOLD SCALP and every mechanic verdict on OMNIGOLD comes from **one replay**, and the only record of when it ran was prose in a comment. `hgOgEvidenceStaleHtml` watches the **gate set** moving under the evidence; `hgOgEvidenceHealthHtml` watches its **thinness**; **neither watches the calendar** — so a reader was gated by a walk of unstated age, presented as current. **`HG_OG_WALK`** (2026-03-29→2026-09-12, 9,897 plans) and **`HG_GOLD_EDGE_WALK`** (2026-07-10→2026-09-10, 2,605 trades) are the machine-readable spans; the age is computed **at render time** against the real clock, whole days floored, and a clock before the walk end reports nothing rather than a negative. **No day count is written into either string.** **No staleness threshold is attached** — nothing here measures gold-edge decay, so a "too old to trade" line would be invented; a 400-day-old walk still just reports 400 days, and nothing gates on it. **`rebake-gold-literals.mjs` rewrites both spans** from the artifacts' meta (a block it cannot find is fatal), so `npm run gold:rebake` keeps them current and the committed tree round-trips to zero drift. Re-bake here is still blocked: 403 CONNECT on every data route. Tests: `tests/test-gold-walk-age.mjs`.
- **ONE POSITION AT A TIME (hg-v926):** Every earlier pack chased *better setups*; hg-v922 measured that **nothing either gold desk ranks by separates outcomes** on four disjoint windows, and the one factor that does (stop width) names no value. So this changes **how many at once**, not which. The walk publishes **59.4 plans/day on one instrument** and holds **57 at once** — not 57 bets, **one bet at 57× size**, which needs no statistics. The other half is the hg-v918 sequential ticket book, baked and never acted on: **SCALP +0.1484R (n=73, lower bound) / +0.4155R (n=54, upper)** — **positive at both fill bounds**, the only read in this desk's evidence that is — against **−0.1160R** all-at-once (n=8132, XM). **The limit is pinned as hard as the claim:** n=73 at **+1.16σ** is positive but **not significant**, clearing not even an uncorrected single test; **SWING disagrees between bounds** (−0.2399 / +0.3166) and carries **no verdict**. While a gold conviction is live — read from **both** booking stores (`hgGoldscalpConviction`, `hgGoldswingConviction`) — a new OMNIGOLD ticket is **HELD**, pushed as an ordinary hard gate via **`hgOgOneAtATimeGate`** so it flows through grading, the funnel and the card. **Fails open:** an unreadable store reads 0 open and the ticket stands. On by default; `hgOgSetOneAtATime(false)` persists, `window.HG_OG_ONE_AT_A_TIME` overrides. No threshold moved. Tests: `tests/test-omnigold-one-at-a-time.mjs`.
- **Measured-edge gate RELAXED, on instruction (hg-v925):** `OG_EDGE_PROOF_DEFAULT = false`. **No evidence was found for this and none is claimed** — the desk owner asked for it after several packs of an empty ticket column, and four earlier loosening arguments were *refused* on measured grounds (hg-v920 grade-A + tally/crowned splits, hg-v922 the 0.28% stop bar, hg-v923 promoting `sweepob`). **Only the middle verdict moves:** a mechanic whose record is **UNKNOWN** no longer stands the setup aside. **The known-failure veto is untouched** — at or below **−2σ** still cannot ticket, in-sample or out. `MIN_SAMPLES` 20, `FWD_MIN_JUDGE` 20, `OG_EFF_N_RATIO` 0.406, the Šidák family bar, the cost ceilings and G1–G7 are all unchanged. **What it frees** (replay, 78 mechanics, bar +3.21σ): 0 of 54 clear, **1 fails outright and stays vetoed**, **53 unknown now ticket**, and **13 with no record at all** now ticket too (including hg-v923's SWEEP-OB) — 66 of 78, against 0 before. **What that cohort measured** over 7,595 settled firings: win **30.5%** vs a **33.3%** breakeven at 2R, gross **−0.0179R**, net **−0.0726R** at XM. Below breakeven before costs; not *significantly* below either, which is why it reads unknown — but not an edge. **The disclosure is not optional:** `hgOgEdgeRelaxedPanelHtml` renders whenever the requirement is off, **first** in the panel chain, with every figure re-derived from the replay at render time (no literal is in the string). **Reversible without an edit:** `hgOgSetEdgeProof(true)` persists; `window.HG_OG_EDGE_PROOF` overrides; `hgOgEdgeProofInit()` runs at mount. Tests: `tests/test-omnigold-edge-relaxed.mjs`.
- **WHY NOTHING TICKETED — OMNIGOLD names the gate that emptied its own board (hg-v924):** GOLD SCALP has had `gsRejectFunnelHTML` (N held back across M gates · binding gate X at P% · per-gate breakdown, plus a WHY SILENT on the fully-empty branch). **OMNIGOLD had no equivalent** — every panel there describes the *rules* or the *replay*, none counts the scan in front of the reader. **`hgOgBlockerFunnelHtml`** counts it: plans scanned, every hard gate that blocked with its share, the binding gate named whenever there is a single one, and the caveat that a plan fails several gates so the counts do not sum. Renders only when **nothing ticketed**. **The cost ceiling is the special case it folds in:** `hgOgVenueCostNoteHtml` fires only when the venue *differs* from the replay's, and the replay is PAXG — so on the fail-closed default the desk said nothing, exactly where the ceiling does the most work (replay: **84.5%** of all plans and **94.7%** of the scalp lane vetoed at PAXG, vs **13.0% / 16.6%** at XM). When `cost-drag` is the binding gate, **`hgOgCostCeilingScanNoteHtml`** adds: vetoed N of M priceable plans, the **sole-blocker** subset of those, and how many of *those* clear the same ceiling re-priced at the other venue. **The denominator is the point** — a plan that also fails confluence is not a ticket the venue withholds, so the counterfactual is scoped to sole blockers (the crypto sole-blocker discipline). Dominance is **measured** from the scan's own ledger; a **tie is two causes** (no binding gate named, no venue paragraph); the tie-break is by name so the answer is stable across renders. The dollar figure is the **median entry of this scan's own plans** — no plans with levels, no price, no dollars. **Nothing is gated on it:** `COST_VETO_R` 0.30, `COST_VETO_R_SCALP` 0.15 and the XM UI venue default are unchanged. Tests: `tests/test-omnigold-cost-emptied.mjs`.
- **SWEEP→OB on OMNIGOLD, and the record it would have inherited (hg-v923):** `hgGoldSweepOb` has run on every gold scan since hg-v560 — HTF location · liquidity raid · MSS · entry on a fresh OB/FVG retrace · stop beyond the sweep extreme + 0.15×ATR · quality ≥7/10 · **R:R ≥ 2.0** · tier-1 news lockout — and mints on GOLD SCALP, but was **never registered as an OMNIGOLD mechanic** (VP got v567, P4 v568, P5 v569, P6 v570, P7 v571; this one was missed). Now wired at six sites, **CONFIRMED path only** — an OMNIGOLD card is a ticket candidate and the watch tier has no target and no R:R. **The defect found while wiring it:** the detector has four early returns that hand back `tier:'watch'` with **no quality**, two of them with no targets and no R:R — honest pre-trigger states, but the GOLD SCALP mint accepts `watch` and mints them under the same stratKey. **All 62 sweepob firings in the committed replay carry a falsy quality — 62 of 62 from an early return, 0 confirmed.** So the `+0.427` gross / `+0.162` net on `HG_GOLD_SETUP_EDGE.scalp.sweepob` and the live `+0.301R` on n=29 are the **PRECURSOR's** record; the confirmed setup has never fired. Every card printed **`Q?/10`** for five months. Each watch return now carries **`stage`**; `hgGoldSweepObStageLabel` names the missing leg; the stamp reads **SWEEP→OB WAITING · <leg>**; the edge row's `why` says PRECURSOR and **`live.precursorOnly` is DERIVED** — a kind stamps a quality on every row and not one is numeric — so `rebake-gold-literals.mjs` emits it and a re-bake that sees one scored firing drops it. (Hand-writing the flag broke the hg-v921 byte-identical guard, which is what that guard is for.) **A second wrong attribution:** `hgOgKindToInstKey` falls back to `vwap` for any unmapped kind — right for gating, wrong for attribution, since a record lookup then hands that mechanic the vwap row (−0.314R, n=132). A **`strict`** second argument returns `null` instead, and **`hgOgSiblingRecordNote`** uses it: the card says NEVER OBSERVED, names the GOLD SCALP record, **attributes** it to that desk and says it measures pre-trigger states. **REFUSED:** the session claim — London/NY overlap 13:00–16:00 UTC is n=233 win 42.9% net −0.047 vs n=972 win **43.4%** net −0.014 *outside* it, and 2 of 4 disjoint windows, so no session gate ships. Also refused: promoting `sweepob` to `prefer` on a record that is not its, and removing the precursor mint (those 29 rows are the best cohort in the scalp book at +0.30R). **This pack re-labelled; it did not re-measure** — n, gross and net are untouched. Register **77 → 78** mechanics, 12 → 13 never-observed, both derived — and registering one **widens the multiple-comparison correction**, which the code already derived: the Sidak family bar moves **3.2091 → 3.2128σ** and expected false promotions per bake 1.93 → 1.95, so every other mechanic's promotion bar gets slightly stricter. Tests: `tests/test-omnigold-sweepob-wire.mjs`, `tests/test-omnigold-unobserved.mjs`.
- **What actually separates winners, and it is not what either desk ranks by (hg-v922):** Twenty signal-time factors, ten per desk, each judged on **four disjoint windows** at **both fill bounds** — a verdict needs all four to agree on **win% AND gross AND net**, at both ends. Win rate and net are always reported together (a win rate rises for free if the target shrinks) and every split sits at planned **R:R 1.49–1.52** on both sides, so no verdict here is a smaller target. **Four of twenty carry a verdict, and none of them is a ranking input.** On the 1,205 trades GOLD SCALP forms today `tally >= 8` is **−7.3 pts win / −0.186R**, `grade A` −3.5 / −0.072, `not demoted` −3.0 / −0.068 — and **none is unanimous in either direction**, so the claim is that the ordering carries no information, not that a low tally is better. OMNIGOLD says the same of its own score (tier STRONG 2/4, confluence≥50 1/4, checks≥4 2/4). **The stop is what separates:** `stop >= 0.28%` and `>= 0.50%` hold on GOLD SCALP at both bounds, `>= 0.50%` on OMNIGOLD — and **69% of the scalp 0.28% net gap is GROSS**, so it is not hg-v919's cost arithmetic restated. **The limit order is the unfavourable verdict:** on OMNIGOLD a pending entry is unanimously **worse** at the as-recorded end (−3.2 pts win, −0.0737 gross, 4,988 of 8,132 rows). The lower bound agrees but is **not neutral here** — `ambiguousSameBarWin` is set only on pending fills (457 of 4,988 limit rows, **0 of 2,875 market rows**), so it demotes one side of that comparison; the verdict rests on the conservative end alone. Adverse selection is the *explanation offered*, not a measured one — the walk's own fill model is the competing one. Published, not acted on, since the alternative pays the spread twice. **No threshold moves.** The direction holds, a number does not: scalp unanimity runs 0.20 no · 0.24 no · **0.28 yes** · 0.32 no · 0.40 no · **0.50 yes** · **0.60 yes**, which is a bar being *searched for*. OMNIGOLD at 0.50% goes −0.116R → **−0.005R** — losing to flat, not positive — while deleting **52%** of volume. **`tier FAIR` (77% of the OMNIGOLD book) is unanimous at the lower bound only** and so carries no verdict; reading one end would have retired three quarters of the desk. Re-derive: `node scripts/factor-separation.mjs`. Tests: `tests/test-factor-separation.mjs`.
- **Re-bake is one command, and routable (hg-v921):** **`npm run gold:rebake`** runs the whole chain — both backtests → `omnigold-evidence-bake --write` → **`rebake-gold-literals --write`** → `npm test`. **`npm run gold:literals`** is the read-only drift check. **Route is a parameter:** both harnesses hardcoded `https://api.binance.com`, which cloud VMs commonly can't reach (HTTP 451); **`lib/klines-source.mjs`** takes **`HG_KLINES_BASE`** (any origin serving `/api/v3/klines`) and **`HG_KLINES_PROXY`** (a HARDGATE origin whose `/api/proxy` fetches it — the `HARDGATE_SITE` escape hatch). They compose; unset, the URL is byte-identical to before, and the harness prints its route. **Literals write themselves:** the five derived blocks — scalp `live` in `goldind.js`, `kinds[5..8]` + `sequentialCells` + `sequentialTicketHorizon` + `stopBandGross` in `omnigold.js` — were hand-transcribed (how hg-v909 shipped a block from the wrong file). The writer preserves **file** key order (artifact order re-sorted the stop bands unreadably), and a block it cannot locate is **fatal, never skipped**. Guard round-trips the committed tree to zero drift, then corrupts both files and rebuilds them byte-identical. Tests: `tests/test-gold-rebake-pipeline.mjs`.
- **Why the gold desks crown almost nothing (hg-v920):** Of everything GOLD SCALP forms, **21.6% suppress · 62.9% demote · 15.5% lead-eligible** — so **84.5% can never be MOST PROBABLE**, 94.1% of settled rows carry a demote, and the six highest-volume detectors (HVN, ORB, BOS align, NY exhaustion, EMA ribbon, Asian breakout) are all demote or suppress. **1,341 grade-A setups are `demoted-A`.** MOST PROBABLE fired 111 times in 5,760 scans — **once in 51**. An empty board is the designed outcome; `goldCoverageNoteHTML` now says so on the **WHY SILENT** panel. **Three arguments for loosening were tested and all refused:** (1) crowned-worse-than-passed-over — true whole-book (−0.231 vs −0.146) but breaks at every OOS split; (2) tally separates within demoted — breaks at 50%, t 1.12/0.68; (3) grade separates — beat B/C at all three splits (+0.065/+0.228/+0.219, t>1.96 twice) **but the splits are NESTED** (70% ⊂ 60% ⊂ 50%), and on four **disjoint** windows grade A is worse in three (−0.379 t=−2.76) and better only in the last, which all three nested sets contained. **`scripts/disjoint-windows.mjs`** replaces the nested procedure used by hg-v914/v915/v916/v918 — every refusal those packs made was re-checked and still stands, so the flaw was latent, not load-bearing. **OMNIGOLD's top tier is unreachable:** band needs 85, scan arithmetic tops out at **78**, 0 of 9,897 rows reached it — the legend now names the number. **Nothing was loosened**; the lead invariant stands (hg-v699 removed a fallback of this shape at −1.49R/trade). Tests: `tests/test-gold-leader-coverage.mjs`.
- **OMNIGOLD cost ceiling is a stop width (hg-v919):** `costR = rtCostPct / stopPct` exactly, so the **0.15R scalp / 0.30R swing** ceilings are a **minimum stop**, and it depends on venue: SCALP **≥0.133% ($6 on $4500 gold) at XM** vs **≥1.733% ($78) at PAXG**; SWING 0.067% ($3) vs 0.867% ($39). Venue-neutral in R by design — not venue-neutral in what it asks of a chart. On the replay the ceiling **alone** vetoes **96.1% of scalp / 47.4% of swing rows at PAXG** against **16.4% / 0.1% at XM** (stable at both fill bounds: 95.9% / 44.4%). **PAXG is the fail-closed default** and `hgOgVenueCostNoteHtml` is silent on it (it only fires when the venue differs from the replay's), so an empty scan reads as a quiet market. **`hgOgCostCeilingDemand`** inverts the ceiling, **`hgOgCostCeilingNote`** states it, the cost-gate veto reason now appends the demand, and **`hgOgCostCeilingPanelHtml`** renders both lanes with what each cost the measured book. **The widest-stop inference is REFUSED:** banded on the sequential book, gross **rises** with stop width at the lower bound (−0.600R → +0.013R) and **falls** at the upper (+0.500R → +0.026R) — tight stops are the trades most likely to resolve on their own fill bar, so the bound swings them hardest. Nothing widens a stop or moves a ceiling. Band edges are the shipped thresholds. Re-derive: `node scripts/omnigold-evidence-bake.mjs [--write]`. Tests: `tests/test-omnigold-cost-ceiling.mjs`.
- **OMNIGOLD sequential book at both bounds (hg-v918):** `sequentialByCell` has been in the committed evidence since the v12 bake and **nothing ever rendered it** — nor `sequentialByKind` / `sequentialTickets`. It walks plans in time order, takes one when flat, holds it to its own exit (this walk forms 59.4/day and holds 57 at once, so every other table describes a book nobody could run). Read alone it says scalp is the problem: **SCALP/FAIR n=210 at −0.343R**. That is the **lower bound** — 1,751 of 7,734 settled rows resolved on their own fill bar, and the lower bound deletes those wins while keeping those losses. `scripts/omnigold-evidence-bake.mjs` already stated the rule: *verdicts must not come from that end*. So the mirror is baked (**`sequentialByCellUpper`**, **`sequentialTicketsByHorizon`** + twin) and a cell counts as losing only when **both ends agree — not one does**: SCALP/FAIR −0.343 / **+0.145**, SCALP/STRONG +0.149 / +0.212, SCALP/WEAK −0.087 / +0.493, SWING/FAIR −0.133 / +0.173. On the **ticket** book one at a time the sign inverts: **SCALP +0.148 / +0.415 (positive at both ends)**, SWING −0.240 / +0.317. **There is no demonstrated scalp loss.** `hgOgSequentialCellsHtml` publishes both ends, last in the panel chain. Nothing is gated on it. Re-derive: `node scripts/omnigold-evidence-bake.mjs [--write]`. Tests: `tests/test-omnigold-sequential-cells.mjs` (runs the generator and requires it to match the committed artifact).
- **OMNIGOLD gate-clear record (hg-v917):** `HG_OG_REPLAY_EVIDENCE.kinds` is `perKind` — every firing of a detector across the whole walk, priced at PAXG — and `hgOgReplayNetAtVenue` re-prices it at render. The same committed `scripts/omnigold-replay-evidence.json` also carries **`sequentialBake.formedByKind`**: the same 54 mechanics scoped to the **gate-clear** population (what cleared the 35-gate stack), `netR_xm` already computed. Nothing read it. **For 42 of the 54 the gate-clear record is WORSE** than the re-priced line — PIN-REJECT −0.21R → **−0.94R**, THREE-BAR −0.24R → **−0.93R**; net-positive at XM falls **12 → 9** and 7 change sign. Rows now carry both (positions 0–4 unscoped as before, **5–8 = `[formedN, formedWinRate, formedNetXm, formedGrossR]`**), rendered as a `gate-clear:` line under the replay line. **No verdict moves:** at naive n nothing reaches the family bar on either basis; one clears the naive 95% bar as displayed and none once scoped (P6-FAIL, +0.0005 → −0.0260). **`EDGE_VETO_SAMPLES` in `omnigold.js` is vestigial** — declared, never read; the gold gate vetoes at `MIN_SAMPLES` (20) per hg-v420, and `omniroute.js` is the file that reads 30. Value left untouched (`gold-forward-read.js` borrows it by name). Re-derive: `node scripts/omnigold-formed-population.mjs`. Tests: `tests/test-omnigold-formed-population.mjs`.
- **Gold edge on the live population (hg-v916):** Every `HG_GOLD_SETUP_EDGE.scalp` n / gross / net is the whole 2,193-trade GOLD SCALP replay, and **45% of that book (988 trades) is no longer formed** — 671 under the hg-v912 cost reject, 470 from `vwap` / `nyexh` / `liqsweep` / `sweep`, which were suppressed *after* the walk so still sit in its main book (153 are both). As the desk forms setups today the same replay reads **n=1205, −0.020R, t=−0.58** (gross **+0.051R**), not n=2193 / −0.148R / t=−5.71. Each row now carries `live: { n, net, oosHeld, oosBroke }` — a suppressed kind is flagged `formsNone` (what the suppression *withholds*), `fvg` / `vwapband` are `live: null` (shadowed before the walk, no population). **`hgGoldEdgeLiveNote`** renders it via `cand.edge.liveWhy` on GOLD ULTRA cards, demote `gateNotes`, OMNIGOLD reasons and GOLD DIRECTION evidence. **No action changed:** `bosalign` (−0.108→+0.012) and `ribbon` (−0.050→+0.027) clear the table's own net<0 demote bar on the live read but fail the walk-forward — bosalign flips sign at all three splits — so both stay demoted and the refusal is recorded in-table. ### MOST PROBABLE levels — hg-v440 / hg-v442
- **Every market-scan tab** pins one **MOST PROBABLE** panel with **ENTRY · STOP · T1 · T2**. CLEAN / confirmed / ticket rows win. Else the best 6/7 NEAR (watch only). Else one closest draft (not a ticket). No levels → no banner. Dual-venue SWING/SCALP merge re-pins the ranked leader. GOLD SCALP / GOLD SWING keep their own eye banners. G1–G7 unchanged.
- Shared helpers: `hgNormalizeSetupRow`, `hgPickMostProbableAny`, `hgMpPin`. Inline scanners note via `cardHTML`. Module desks call `hgMpPin` after paint.
- Tests: `tests/test-most-probable-panel.mjs`, `tests/test-most-probable-all-tabs.mjs`.

### Crypto desks scan themselves — hg-v439
- Opening **SWING / SCALP / EDGE / BEST** starts that desk's scan. A busy BRAIN or 10-min cycle **retries** the tab-open scan instead of dropping it. Background `hgScanAllTabs` passes `quiet` so those four use `cryptoScanWarm` / `bestScanWarm` / `edgeWarm` and do not steal the RUN button. EDGE still auto-runs on the visible pane after a headless warm. G1–G7 unchanged.
- Tests: `tests/test-crypto-tab-auto-scan.mjs`.

### Quiet liquid scan warm — hg-v438
- Ship stamp for bounded SWING/SCALP/EDGE/BEST scans + quiet warm (`HG_SCAN_WARM_N = 80`) merged onto `main` with the spot-membership gate (a perp is not a spot pair). G1–G7 unchanged.
- Tests: `tests/test-scan-universe-bound.mjs`, `tests/test-dated-futures-gate.mjs`.

### Quiet liquid scan warm — hg-v437
- Alert / pine / SUPER SETUP / silent BEST warm uses **`HG_SCAN_WARM_N = 80`**, not `forceScanAll`. Quiet SWING/SCALP loops do not write `#swingStat` / `#scalpStat` or the progress bar. Quiet `runCascadeCore` does not write `#bestStat` (so a background BEST warm cannot look like `gates 80/80`). `opts.n` wins over the SCAN TOP input.
- Dual venue is still both books at the configured depth. SCAN WHOLE EXCHANGE stays an explicit checkbox. G1–G7 unchanged.
- Tests: `tests/test-scan-universe-bound.mjs`.

### Bounded crypto scans — hg-v435
- Dual venue still means **Delta + CoinDCX**. It does **not** mean SCAN WHOLE EXCHANGE. SWING/SCALP/BEST default to top N by turnover; CoinDCX tickers merge marks so that N is liquid. EDGE `MAX_UNIVERSE` is 80. G1–G7 unchanged.
- Tests: `tests/test-scan-universe-bound.mjs`.

### OMNIGOLD XM bot backtest — hg-v434
- **BACKTEST BOT** on the OMNIGOLD XM panel replays the **send path**, not the mechanic R/HORIZON GRID (that enters at bar close). Universe is `ogXmTicketOk` TICKET rows; fill is pending at setup entry (`xmOrderType`); same-bar SL+T1 is STOP; unfilled is not a loss; GROSS and NET of the $0.30×2 gold spread.
- In-sample on bars the scan already fetched. Session/killzone read each prefix bar; macro/news use the last scan snapshot. Not a live XM statement. Does not POST to XM. Crypto execute stays disabled.
- Tests: `node tests/test-omnigold-xm-bot-backtest.mjs`.

### OMNIGOLD XM bot — hg-v433
- Dedicated gold-lot path: OMNIGOLD **TICKET** rows → `POST /api/xm/order`. Default DRY RUN. Live requires `XM_OMNIGOLD_LIVE=1` + `HARDGATE_API_SECRET`. WATCH/VETO never send. Crypto execute / daemon CCXT stay disabled.
- Tests: `node tests/test-omnigold-xm-bot.mjs`.

### Setup honesty pack — hg-v430
- **MOST PROBABLE** prefers tape-aligned, post-gate-**checked** 7/7 over a hotter UNCHECKED or against-tape row (`hgRankCryptoSetups`). Crypto CLEAN scans stamp `POST-GATE UNCHECKED` when flow/RS could not run (gold already did).
- SUPER SETUP: NEAR is **watch only**, never a "ticket". CLEAN without min-loss is **audit hold**. Hydrate kicks async post-gate. SUPER BEST lite enrich is **SIZE OK**, not MIN LOSS PASS (full audit still required).
- OMNIPRESENT measured-edge: a mild positive at 20+ samples is **UNCHECKED** until it clears the 2-mechanic bar; a losing 20+ sample mechanic still VETOES.
- Heuristic chart-vision formation boost no longer promotes (Gemini still can). G1–G7, gold min-loss, OMNIPRESENT one-side, TRIGGERED=live / ARMED=WATCH unchanged.
- Tests: `node tests/test-setup-honesty-v430.mjs`.

### OMNIPRESENT one side per contract — hg-v429
- One card per **base + venue**. When the desk call is **TAKE LONGS** / **TAKE SHORTS**, that direction wins even if the other side is TRIGGERED or nearer. The shown head does not mix LONG and SHORT on the same name (the BZ CoinDCX pair).
- If the tape has a side and there is no with-tape zone, the head is empty — we do not substitute the against-tape card. Alerts / forward-log follow the shown head.
- Tests: `node tests/test-omnipresent-one-side.mjs`.

### OMNIROUTE / OMNIPRESENT market side — hg-v428
- Both desks print **TAKE LONGS** / **TAKE SHORTS** / **STAND ASIDE** from two gates (not a score): **tape** = MARKET PICTURE majority (4H EMA cascade on BTC/ETH/SOL/GOLD); **sentiment** = BIAS S2 Fear & Greed (block fresh longs at ≥80, fresh shorts at ≤20). Extreme sentiment stands aside — it does not flip you to the other side. Missing F&G is UNCHECKED and does not veto a clear tape.
- Cards against the side still render, stamped **AGAINST TAPE**. The banner is not a ticket. Header MARKET PICTURE stays informational; the desks make the call. Chrome-min does not hide the banner.
- Shared engine: `hgOmniMarketSide` / `hgOmniMarketSideHtml` in `omniroute.js`. Picture cached as `window.__hgMarketPicture`.
- Tests: `node tests/test-omni-market-side.mjs`.

### Android app — hg-v427
- The desk is **not rewritten in Kotlin**. `android/` is a full-screen WebView launcher over **https://hardgate-main.onrender.com** (JS on, HTTPS only, no `JavascriptInterface`, no file://). A Render deploy is the next app launch.
- Sideload APK from GitHub Actions **Android APK** (`assembleDebug` → `app-debug.apk`, id `app.hardgate.desk.debug`). Do not commit a keystore.
- Chrome **Install app** uses `icon-192.png` + `icon-512.png` in `manifest.webmanifest`.
- Tests: `node tests/test-android-app.mjs`. See `android/README.md`.

### Header chrome key — hg-v426
- Press **backtick** or the header **MIN `** button to collapse the tools drawer, MARKET PICTURE, status chips, group chips, brand tagline, and `.hg-lead` tab intros so setup cards fill the window. Tab nav and venue toggles stay. Press again to restore.
- Persists in `localStorage` `hg_chrome_min`. Ignored while focus is in an input / textarea / select.
- Desktop always-open drawer yields (`html:not(.hg-chrome-min)`). Tests: `node tests/test-chrome-min.mjs`.

### OMNIROUTE keep last scan — hg-v425
- A finished OMNIROUTE scan **stays on screen** until a newer scan successfully replaces it. Rescans no longer blank `#omniCards` first.
- Tab-open / `hardRefreshAll` go through `refreshOmniroute` (skip when **busy** or **fresh &lt; 3 min**). `#omniRun` click is only the first-run fallback.
- A failed rescan **keeps the last cards** and names the error on the warn line (`rescan failed — keeping last scan`).
- Tests: `node tests/test-omniroute-keep-results.mjs`, `node tests/test-scan-stability.mjs`

### OMNIROUTE setup levels — hg-v424
- Tickets **are the setup**. `hgOmniPlanForHit` prices **ENTRY at `hit.level`** (ORB / FVG / VALUE), not last close. Sweeps and reversions stop beyond that level (crypto cap **6×ATR**, not gold 2.5%). Continuation uses structure **from that entry** (`skipExact`); fades never get a momentum stop.
- Cards print **SETUP {kind} @ {level}**. `hgOmniConsensusVoters` drops TREND hits the daily stack already disqualifies (no-op when HTF is absent, so existing consensus harnesses stay two-sided).
- **OMNIPRESENT is unchanged:** TRIGGERED still enters at the **live** print after rejection; ARMED stays WATCH at the zone edge. Do not copy `hgOmniPlanForHit` onto that desk.
- G1–G7, gold min-loss, and OMNIGOLD v423 setup-level tickets stay.
- Tests: `node tests/test-omniroute-setup-levels.mjs`, `node tests/test-omniroute.mjs`, `node tests/test-omniroute-consensus.mjs`

### OMNIGOLD setup levels — hg-v423
- Tickets **are the setup**. `hgOgPlanForHit` prices **ENTRY at `hit.level`** (ROUND-MAGNET @ 4530, FVG @ 4429), not live gold. Sweeps stop beyond that level; continuation still uses structure / labelled vol-stop **from that entry** (`skipExact`). Stops clip at **2.5% of gold** — a 1000-pt lastSwing is not a gold invalidation.
- Cards print **SETUP {kind} @ {level}**. `hgOgConsensusVoters` drops continuation/fade hits the daily stack already disqualifies, so rejected TREND shorts cannot empty a with-trend scalp.
- `hgOgPickFor` still prefers structure then vol-stop; among those, prefers tickets **≤ 2×ATR** from live gold so a far FVG does not float as STRONGEST over a nearby sweep.
- **Do not** run OMNIGOLD through `hgApplyGoldBestLevels`. G1–G7 and v420 min-loss vetoes stay. `momentum-stop` stays AGAINST (v422).
- Tests: `node tests/test-omnigold-setup-levels.mjs`, `node tests/test-gold-live-entry.mjs`, `node tests/test-omnigold-consensus.mjs`

### OMNIGOLD tickets — hg-v422
- Continuation with a labelled **volatility / momentum stop** can **TICKET** (`momentum-stop` is AGAINST, not a veto). Killing it as a real veto emptied the desk on a runaway gold trend (no nearby structure for with-trend; fades already stood aside).
- `hgOgPickFor` prefers a **structural** ticket; if the only ticket is a vol-stop continuation, it still picks that rather than leaving STRONGEST empty.
- Min-loss vetoes from hg-v420 **stay**: fade daily-stack, weekend closure, yield-per-direction, 20-sample losing mechanic, ShieldGuard, scalp cost-drag 0.15R.
- Tests: `node tests/test-omnigold-min-loss.mjs`, `node tests/test-momentum-stop.mjs`

### OMNIGOLD min-loss — hg-v420
- **Do not** run OMNIGOLD through `hgApplyGoldBestLevels`. The desk tickets **the named setup level** (`hgOgPlanForHit`); snapping to formation levels recreates DEAD ON ARRIVAL / stale-entry tickets.
- Yield is judged **per setup direction** (`hgOgYieldValid(rows, dir)`). Never freeze `validateYieldCorrelation(..., 'long')` for the whole scan.
- A mechanic at **20+ samples and ≤ −2σ vs breakeven VETOES** (no 20–29 info free-pass). OmniRoute crypto keeps the 20–29 AGAINST window.
- Fading the **daily stack** is enough to veto (`fade-strength`). A volatility / momentum stop is **AGAINST** (`info`) — the continuation ticket stands, otherwise a runaway tape has no nearby structure. `hgOgPickFor` prefers a structural ticket, then falls back to the labelled vol-stop rather than leaving STRONGEST empty.
- **Weekend:** inside Fri 22:00 UTC–Sun 22:00 UTC is a veto; SWING also vetoes inside one 4h bar of the Friday close. Scalp cost-drag ceiling is **0.15R** (`sessionHard`).
- Tests: `node tests/test-omnigold-min-loss.mjs`, `node tests/test-omnigold.mjs`, `node tests/test-fade-strength.mjs`, `node tests/test-momentum-stop.mjs`

### OMNIPRESENT max-quality — hg-v421
- **TICKET** only on **TRIGGERED** (1h close rejected the zone). ARMED is WATCH — hard UNCHECKED `rejection` gate, not a veto badge.
- Confluence **< 3** and exhaustion **< 2** are hard vetoes (the tab already claimed this; AGAINST notes used to still ticket).
- Daily EMA stack against the fade vetoes (`htf-daily`). Running ADX without **RSI divergence** vetoes (`trend-guard`) — stretch is the trend.
- Adverse context panel (`cx.adverse`) is a real veto. Settled `OP-HIGH-REJECT` / `OP-LOW-REJECT` at 20+ samples with negative expectancy vetoes.
- TICKET cards carry ADD TO BOOK + SEND TO TRADE PLAN. Tab open auto-scans (`HG_TAB_AUTO_SCAN.omnipresent`).
- Tests: `node tests/test-omnipresent-max.mjs`, `node tests/test-omnipresent.mjs`

### Auto hard refresh — hg-v441
- **`HG_GLOBAL_SCAN_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000`** — every **10 minutes**, always. `hardRefreshAll()` → `hgScanAllTabs()` runs every scan in every nav tab / subtab / `HG_TAB_MODS.refresh()` (including GOLD SCALP/SWING and SUPER desks). The 10-min alert cycle uses the same sweep so it cannot sneak a 5-min side door.
- **`HG_AUTO_REFRESH_HARDCODED_MS = 0`** — the header AUTO selector is **UNLOCKED**: OFF / 2m / 3m / 5m / 10m / 15m clicks are **honoured**, and `localStorage hgAutoRefresh` stores what was pressed. *(This line claimed the constant equalled `HG_GLOBAL_SCAN_MS`, that the header showed AUTO 10m and that those clicks were ignored. hg-v656 set the constant to 0 and did not correct it here, so the file described a lock the source had already removed — corrected in hg-v965, which found it while adding a clock of its own.)*
- **GOLD SCALP additionally runs on its own 3-minute clock** (`HG_GOLDSCALP_AUTO_MS`, hg-v965) — an extra clock for one desk, not a change to the sweep above.
- Quiet candle TTL is **720s** so cache spans the 10-min boundary. G1–G7 unchanged.
- Tests: `tests/test-scan-every-10m.mjs`, `tests/test-hard-refresh.mjs`.

### Desk formation edge — hg-v609
- **OMNIROUTE BEST kinds** (AVWAP-RECLAIM, CUSUM-SHIFT, DONCHIAN-DRIVE, MMOVE, NR7-BREAK — the only v531 net-positive book, +0.10R prefer-only vs −0.24R overall) retune formation on **SWING SCAN, SCALP SCAN, EDGE, SMART $, SQUEEZE, REVERSAL SNIPER, SMC (FVG), ORDER BLOCKS, LIQUIDITY TRAP, DIVERGENCE, COIL, APEX, OI FLOW, LIQS, ON-CHAIN, CHART VISION, CARRY, VENUE, TERM BASIS**.
- **What changes:** DIVERGENCE / SMC FVG / **REVERSAL SNIPER** **suppress** trade-ready tickets (RSI-DIVERGE −0.62R, FVG-FILL −0.19R, PIN-REJECT −1.03R). TRAP **demote** (sweep family). SCALP + coil-expansion **prefer** NR7-BREAK. Fail-open desks get a BEST-confirm rank boost when a prefer kind or catalog tally agrees. Nightly asides can tighten further.

- Tests: `tests/test-desk-formation-edge.mjs`. Overlay: `desk-formation-edge.js`.

### Nightly formation rebake — hg-v610
- **Every night after 21:00 UTC** (London close) a rolling **960 closed 1h bars** (~40 days; the first-night 120-bar walk never hit n≥8) retune **OMNIROUTE / OMNIPRESENT / OMNIGOLD 1** and the **19 desk tabs** (SWING / SCALP / EDGE / SMART $ / SQUEEZE / SNIPER / SMC / OB / TRAP / DIV / COIL / APEX / OI FLOW / LIQS / ON-CHAIN / CHART VISION / CARRY / VENUE / TERM BASIS). Scheduler: `scripts/formation-nightly-watch.mjs` on Render + `.github/workflows/formation-nightly.yml` backup. Overlay: `scripts/formation-nightly.json` served at `GET /api/formation-nightly`.
- **What changes:** day-toxic kinds (n≥8, gross≤−0.05 or net≤−0.20) **stand aside**; day-survivors (gross+ and net+) **prefer**; matching desk analogues **tighten** (watch→suppress, prefer→suppress) and BEST-confirm drops aside kinds; OMNIPRESENT cost ceiling may **tighten** (never above baked 0.12); a toxic fade day stands TRIGGERED aside (ARMED stays WATCH); OMNIGOLD 1 picks the day's least-bad tighten-only filter (`raw` / `minRisk5` / `minDisp0.5` / `minRisk5+disp0.5`) but **never** drops SL$ &lt; $5 or displacement &lt; 0.5×ATR.
- **What does not change:** G1–G7, OMNIPRESENT 3+/2+, baked OMNIROUTE demotes, baked desk suppress/demote (DIVERGENCE / SMC / TRAP / SNIPER), gold-perp aside. A green day-prefer cannot un-suppress a baked-toxic desk. Never invents direction or tickets. `gated+bias` is not a QUALIFIES gate.
- Tests: `tests/test-formation-nightly.mjs`. Rebake: `node scripts/nightly-formation-rebake.mjs --bars=960`.

### Replay-guided setup tighten — hg-v607
- **Backtest evidence** from committed books (`scripts/backtest-omnigold-results.json`, `backtest-omniroute-v531-results.json`, `backtest-omnipresent-results.json`) plus a new **OMNIGOLD 1** replay harness (`scripts/backtest-omnigold1.mjs`) that walks `hgOg1Replay` with zero lookahead.
- **GOLD SCALP / SWING:** ORB / FVG / HVN / ribbon stay **suppress**. VWAP / Asian / scalp sweep / BOS / OB stay **demote** (never MOST PROBABLE or OMNIGOLD ENGINE lead). SWING 4H sweep + weekly stay **prefer**.
- **OMNIROUTE:** demote also when n≥50, gross&lt;0 and net≤−0.20 (catches **PO3**). ORB / VALUE still form. Prefer list unchanged (AVWAP-RECLAIM, CUSUM-SHIFT, DONCHIAN-DRIVE, MMOVE, NR7-BREAK).
- **OMNIPRESENT:** cost ceiling **0.20 → 0.12**. Gold perps still stand aside. 3+/2+ gates not loosened.
- **OMNIGOLD 1:** QUALIFIES needs SL$ ≥ $5 (XM 0.125R cost floor); sweep G5 fails closed without ≥0.5×ATR displacement; BEST / MOST PROBABLE prefer trade-ready; forward log records only trade-ready / qualifying. PAXG 1h replay (2000 bars): SL$≥$5 + disp 0.5 is least-bad (−0.245R vs raw −0.275R); gated+bias worse — attached as measured lines, not a QUALIFIES gate.
- Never loosen G1–G7. Never invent direction. Tests: `tests/test-gold-setup-edge.mjs`, `tests/test-omni-replay-edge.mjs`, `tests/test-omnigold1.mjs`.

### Gold 7-step setup engine — hg-v594
- **`gold-seven-step.js`** — Playbook Parts 1–9 + Master Catalog readout on **OMNIGOLD / GOLD SCALP / GOLD SWING** (`hgGoldSevenStep(inp)` → `hgGoldSevenStepHtml` / `hgGoldSevenStepText` / `hgGoldSevenStepPanel`). STEP 1 indicators + strategy eligibility → STEP 2 4H bias (LONG / SHORT / BOTH / NO TRADE, RSI veto) → STEP 3 candidates ranked by **rule-based confluence rank** (gates passed · location grade · RR · one vote per family) → STEP 4 best fit (hard vetoes, ≥10/12 core gates or NO SETUP) → STEP 5 entry/stop/T1/T2/size/venue basis → STEP 6 checklist VALID / VALID-HALF / INVALID + sanity a–f → STEP 7 **TRIGGERED / WAIT / EXPIRED** on the last closed 1H bar.
- **Closed candles only** (`hgGoldSevenStepClosedRows`); 4H derived from 1H aligned to **22:00 UTC** when the feed has no 4H leg. Stale feed (> 2h), < 60 × 1H bars, day-sized hole, or venue basis > ±1.5% → **DATA_UNAVAILABLE** and stop. Missing legs print **unavailable** — never estimated. **No win rate / probability / confidence %.** IST with UTC in brackets; DST via `Intl`.
- Desks pass `tape` (same `hgGoldUniformTape` / `__og.tape.desk` read as the uniform card); against-tape candidates are **HELD**, never best fit. `hgGoldSevenStepLoad1h(400)` fetches the 1H leg (GOLD SWING adds it; GOLD SCALP deepens its 200-bar leg; OMNIGOLD paints from 15m→1H then swaps in the 400-bar leg). Tests: **`tests/test-gold-seven-step.mjs`**.

### OMNIGOLD 1 — institutional 20-point matrix — hg-v595
- **`omnigold1.js`** — GOLD → **OMNIGOLD 1** tab (`hgOg1Engine(inp)` → `hgOg1Html` / `hgOg1Text`; state `omnigold1State()`). Sections 0–8 in order: **data load + 10-item veto stack** (any veto → SCORE 0 · NO TRADE), **regime/permission** (KER TREND/MIXED/CHOP class enablement, day type, 4H bias, weekly S28 permission, trader state), **20-point matrix** (Block A 10 · Block B 10, one vote per family, 4-family spread rule), **decision** (≥12 full · 10–11 half if RR ≥ 2 · ML inflation guard), **strategy selector** (S0–S66 classes + priority ladder; equal-rank conflict → WAIT), **levels** (stop buffer rules · TP2 caps · S41 expression), **V-Mod sizing** (ATR100/ATR14 clamped 0.5–1.5 · tier · trader · venue multiplier · liquidation clearance), **12-gate cross-check** (gates win → MATRIX/GATE CONFLICT), **trigger** TRIGGERED / WAIT / EXPIRED.
- Reuses **`HG_GOLD7`** helpers exported by `gold-seven-step.js` (closed bars, VP, pools, sweeps, gates, sessions, IST). Inputs the app cannot fetch (GVZ, COT, real yields, FedWatch, CVD, weekly physical reads, ML, hour histogram) come from the tab's **DATA BLOCK** JSON textarea; everything else prints **unavailable**. Account / base risk / trader state / stops persist in `localStorage` (`hg_gold_equity`, `hg_og1_*`).
- Against-tape hypotheses are HELD, never scored best. Tests: **`tests/test-omnigold1.mjs`**.
